On Qwen3-8B, SSPO consistently outperforms GRPO across BrowseComp, GAIA, and FRAMES, surpassing or matching GRPO trained with twice as many gradient steps while adding only about 5 percent overhead per step from a single additional forward pass.

</details>


### [106] [CoMedBench: A Multi-Source Benchmark of Synthetic Medical Data Fidelity and Downstream Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12805)
*Akanta Das,Al Amin Farhad,Mrinmoy Sarkar Anto,David Rehkopf,Ayin Vala,Tanmoy Sarkar Pias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoMedBench是一个可复现的基准测试框架，用于在统一临床有效性框架下评估多种合成数据生成器在静态表格和时序ICU下游任务中的表现，涵盖7个公开数据源的37个数据集-任务对，对比真实与合成数据训练模型的性能，发现表格任务中合成数据保留了90%以上的信号（最好生成器CoMed-TVAE达97.3% AUROC），而时序ICU任务更难且对生成器敏感（AUROC保留81.6%，AUPRC仅64%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗数据隐私法规（如隐私监管、IRB审核、数据使用协议）限制了对电子健康记录的直接访问，合成数据是潜在替代方案，但已有研究多为单一生成器、单数据集或窄下游任务的评估，缺乏跨生成器、跨任务、跨数据集的系统比较，难以判断合成数据何时足以支撑模型开发、何时丢失任务关键信号。

Method: 构建CoMedBench可复现基准，在统一临床有效性框架和共享的训练/评估引擎下，同时评估一族生成器；覆盖静态表格与时序ICU两类下游任务，共37个数据集-任务对（20个静态表格、17个时序），来源包括MIMIC-III/IV、eICU、UCI、CDC BRFSS、NHANES及pycox生存数据；通过比较真实数据与合成数据上训练模型的性能来评估统计保真度和任务效用。

Result: 表格任务中合成训练数据保留大部分下游信号：参考生成器CoMed-CTGAN平均AUROC效用（合成/真实性能比）为90.6%，最强生成器CoMed-TVAE达97.3%；时序ICU任务更具挑战且对生成器更敏感，CoMed-CTGAN保留81.6%的AUROC但仅保留64.0%的AUPRC，而CoMed-TVAE仍保留约95%的AUROC。

Conclusion: 合成数据在不同模态和任务上的实用性存在显著差异：静态表格任务中合成数据能较好地保留任务信号，而时序ICU任务（尤其在不平衡敏感的AUPRC指标下）对生成器选择高度敏感，提示在部署合成数据前需在具体下游任务上进行严格的效用验证。

Abstract: Access to clinical data is essential for developing reliable healthcare machine learning systems, but direct use of electronic health records is constrained by privacy regulation, institutional review, data-use agreements, and the risk of re-identification. Synthetic data promises a practical alternative: it can preserve useful statistical and clinical structure while reducing exposure of sensitive patient records. Prior studies often evaluate a single generator, one dataset, or a narrow downstream task, making it difficult to know when synthetic data can support model development and when it fails to preserve task-critical signal. We introduce CoMedBench, a reproducible benchmark that evaluates a family of generators under a common clinical-validity framework and one shared training and evaluation engine, spanning static tabular and temporal downstream tasks on established critical-care datasets. In total the benchmark spans 37 dataset-task pairs across two modalities consists of 20 static tabular and 17 temporal ICU time-series-drawn from seven public data sources: three intensive-care databases (MIMIC-III, MIMIC-IV, and eICU) together with the UCI Machine Learning Repository, the CDC BRFSS diabetes cohort (2015), NHANES (1999-2014), and the pycox survival datasets (GBSG and METABRIC). The benchmark evaluates both statistical fidelity and task utility by comparing models trained and tested across real and synthetic data. In these settings, synthetic training data preserves most of the downstream signal: on tabular tasks the reference generator CoMed-CTGAN retains a mean AUROC utility (the synthetic-to-real performance ratio) of 90.6%, rising to 97.3% for the strongest generator, CoMed-TVAE. Temporal ICU tasks are harder and more generator-sensitive: CoMed-CTGAN retains 81.6% (AUROC) and only 64.0% under the imbalance-sensitive AUPRC, whereas CoMed-TVAE still retains ~95% (AUROC).

</details>


### [107] [HiRoute: Hierarchical Routed Prompt Tuning for Safety Alignment of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12821)
*Fangzhou Chen,Shiji Zhao,Mengyang Wang,Qihui Zhu,Ranjie Duan,Maoxun Yuan,Xingxing Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HiRoute是一个输入自适应的层次化提示词微调框架，通过轻量级层次路由器实现类别无关的安全控制与类别特定的响应引导分离，从而在不牺牲响应有用性的前提下提升大语言模型的安全性并减少过度拒绝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于提示词微调的参数高效安全对齐方法通常依赖单一全局提示或外部选择的提示模块，这些静态设计难以在跨类别安全边界与针对特定风险的构造性响应之间取得平衡，同时避免对良性输入的过度拒绝。

Method: HiRoute首先在冻结的LLM表示上训练一个轻量级层次路由器，联合检测有害意图并预测多标签风险分数；然后冻结骨干模型和路由器，使用带有交替梯度更新的偏好优化来学习共享的粗粒度提示和一组细粒度提示专家（作为连续嵌入）。推理时良性输入绕过安全分支，而风险输入使用共享提示与路由器加权混合的风险特定提示专家进行处理。

Result: 在三个指令微调模型上的实验表明，HiRoute在多个安全基准上实现了高安全率，同时保持了安全响应的有用性，减少了过度拒绝，并在通用任务上保持有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: HiRoute通过将类别无关的安全控制与类别特定的响应引导相分离，实现了输入自适应的安全对齐，有效兼顾了安全性与有用性，为参数高效的安全对齐提供了一种新的层次化提示调优范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to harmful requests and jailbreak attacks. Parameter-efficient safety alignment methods based on prompt tuning typically rely on a single global prompt or externally selected prompt modules. Such static designs struggle to maintain a cross-category safety boundary while generating constructive responses tailored to specific risks and avoiding over-refusal of benign inputs. To address these limitations, we propose HiRoute, an input-adaptive hierarchical prompt-tuning framework that separates category-agnostic safety control from category-specific response guidance. HiRoute first trains a lightweight hierarchical router on representations extracted from a frozen LLM to jointly detect harmful intent and predict multi-label risk scores. It then freezes both the backbone model and the router and uses preference optimization with alternating gradient updates to learn a shared coarse-grained prompt and a set of fine-grained prompt experts as continuous embeddings. At inference time, benign inputs bypass the safety branch, whereas risky inputs are processed using the shared prompt together with a router-weighted mixture of risk-specific prompt experts. Experiments across three instruction-tuned models show that HiRoute achieves high safety rates across multiple safety benchmarks while preserving safe-response helpfulness, reducing over-refusal, and maintaining competitive performance on general-purpose tasks.

</details>


### [108] [Fast A/B/n Testing: Exact Multi-Policy Comparison via Tree-Coupled Feedback Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12831)
*Yuxiao Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出树耦合A/B测试（Tree-Coupled A/B Testing, \TCAB），一种支持任意历史依赖上下文赌博机策略进行精确反馈共享的实验设计。其核心思想是通过预测树将策略历史耦合连接，使奖励查询次数从独立的JT降至T+o(T)（在特定条件下），同时保持每个策略的独立有限时域轨迹分布不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线平台常需比较多种自适应决策策略（如排序系统、推荐算法、定价规则、语言模型智能体等），但每次带奖励的交互可能有成本或风险。传统的A/B/n设计需要给J个策略各自分配长度为T的轨迹，总共消耗JT个结果，代价高昂。

Method: \TCAB设计在每轮通过一棵预测树连接当前各策略的历史；每个父子上下文-动作分布被最大化耦合，且匹配树边的组件间共享一个奖励。定义D_{e,t}记录树边e在第t轮的不匹配情况，则奖励查询数满足路径恒等式N(T)=T+\sum_{t,e}D_{e,t}。该成本在选定的树上，对于精确边局部设计是条件最优的；当前轮的最小生成树在树设计中是近视最优的。

Result: 对于固定的J，若每个策略具有次线性伪遗憾且神谕动作几乎必然唯一，则\mathbb{E}[N(T)]=T+o(T)，而独立运行为JT。文章还给出了成对策略对比的有限样本方差界。实验在奖励模型评估、多选语言模型评估和自适应搜索策略上展示了成本-精度前沿的显著提升。

Conclusion: \TCAB提供了一种在保持每个策略精确独立轨迹分布的同时，通过智能耦合大幅减少奖励查询次数的实验设计框架，在理论和实验层面均证明了其在成本-精度权衡上的优越性。

Abstract: Online platforms increasingly compare many adaptive decision policies---ranking systems, recommendation algorithms, pricing rules, and language-model agents---while each reward-bearing interaction can be costly or risky. A direct A/B/n design gives each of $J$ policies its own horizon-$T$ trajectory and therefore uses $JT$ outcomes. We introduce Tree-Coupled A/B Testing (\TCAB), an exact feedback-sharing design for arbitrary history-dependent contextual-bandit policies. At each round, a predictable tree connects the current policy histories; every parent--child context--action law is maximally coupled, and one reward is shared within each component of matched tree edges. Every policy retains exactly its standalone finite-horizon trajectory law, even though the policies are deliberately dependent. If $D_{e,t}$ records a mismatch on tree edge $e$ at round $t$, the number of reward queries satisfies the pathwise identity $N(T)=T+\sum_{t,e}D_{e,t}$ and hence equals $T$ plus cumulative tree-edge total variation in expectation. This cost is conditionally optimal among exact edge-local designs on the selected tree, and a current-round minimum-spanning tree is myopically optimal among tree designs. For fixed $J$, sublinear pseudo-regret of every policy and almost-sure uniqueness of the oracle action imply $\mathbb{E}[N(T)]=T+o(T)$, versus $JT$ for independent runs. We also obtain finite-sample variance bounds for pairwise policy contrasts. Experiments on reward-model evaluation, multiple-choice language-model evaluation, and adaptive search policies demonstrate substantial improvements in the cost--precision frontier.

</details>


### [109] [A Compositional Theory of Curvature in Probabilistic Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12869)
*Hrithik Suresh,Sahil Sidheekh,Shelar Parth Vijay,Yasir Z,Sriraam Natarajan,Narayanan Chatapuram Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对概率电路（PCs）的Hessian迹正则化问题展开研究，证明全局sharpness正则化在PCs上存在设定不当的问题，提出一种基于节点局部曲率的自适应sharpness正则化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCs作为生成模型支持精确推断和Hessian迹的精确计算，但已有工作将sharpness作为全局正则化器引入学习，作者指出这种全局视角在PCs上存在misspecification问题，因为PC的曲率本质上具有组合结构（compository），需要更精细的处理。

Method: 首先从理论上证明每个求和节点对Hessian迹的贡献可以精确分解为电路流（circuit flow，衡量节点使用频率）与由输出分布决定的局部sharpness项的乘积。基于该分解揭示全局sharpness正则化存在深度偏差（depth bias）并可能导致欠拟合，进而提出自适应的sharpness感知正则化器，按节点固有局部曲率加权惩罚，同时保持EM更新的闭式解。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的目标正则化方法能够恢复全局正则化所牺牲的泛化性能，同时保留sharpness感知学习的鲁棒性和优势。

Conclusion: 全局sharpness正则化在PCs上存在深度偏差问题，而利用Hessian迹的因子分解设计节点级的自适应局部曲率正则化，可以在保持EM闭式更新的同时取得更好的泛化与鲁棒性平衡。

Abstract: Probabilistic Circuits (PCs) are generative models that support exact inference and, unlike deep neural networks, admit an exact and tractable measure of loss-surface curvature: the trace of the Hessian of the log-likelihood. Recent work regularizes this trace globally to bias learning toward flatter, better generalizing optima. We show that treating sharpness as a global regularizer can be misspecified for PCs, whose curvature is inherently compositional. We prove that each sum node's contribution to the Hessian trace factorizes exactly into its circuit flow, which measures how heavily the node is used, and a local sharpness term determined by its output distribution. This decomposition provides insights into why global sharpness regularization is depth biased and can lead to underfitting. Building on it, we introduce an adaptive sharpness aware regularizer that penalizes nodes based on intrinsic local curvature and preserves closed form EM updates. We also show that empirically, this targeted regularization recovers the generalization that global regularization sacrifices while retaining the robustness and benefits of sharpness aware learning.

</details>


### [110] [Sustaining Plasticity via Learnable Wavelet Activations in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12874)
*Zeyang Zhang,Tieliang Gong,Junyan Lu,Weizhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可学习的小波激活函数，将激活函数分解为低频和高频分量以对抗频谱偏置，结合动态小波注入和正则化策略，在持续学习中同时增强可塑性并保持稳定性，理论上提供了L2逼近的结构必要性证明和退耦学习率机制恢复高频可塑性的数学保证，实验在多个持续学习基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习中的可塑性损失（plasticity loss）严重阻碍顺序任务获取。现有固定形式激活函数对小波低频变化存在固有频谱偏置，而可学习变体又允许无约束更新导致灾难性遗忘。作者旨在解决这一两难困境：既要对抗频谱偏置提升可塑性，又要防止可学习参数的过度更新破坏旧知识。

Method: 提出可学习小波激活函数（learnable wavelet activation），将激活函数分解为低频和高频两部分以显式对抗频谱偏置。具体包括：(1) 动态小波注入机制自适应增强新任务的可塑性；(2) 正则化策略保证先前知识的稳定性；(3) 退耦学习率机制分别调控低/高频分量的学习速率；此外还设计了基于损失驱动的注入触发机制来精确引导注入时机。理论上给出了混合小波架构的结构必要性与L2逼近效率的严格数学证明。

Result: 大量实证评估表明该方法在整个学习过程中保持优越的可训练性和泛化能力，在多个多样的持续学习基准上达到最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过小波域的低/高频分解与动态注入机制，成功在持续学习中实现了可塑性与稳定性的平衡，既解决了频谱偏置问题又避免了灾难性遗忘，并有严格理论保障。

Abstract: Plasticity loss has emerged as a critical challenge in continual learning that significantly hinders the acquisition of sequential tasks. While optimizing activation designs offers a potential solution, current fixed-form functions suffer from an inherent spectral bias towards low-frequency variations, whereas learnable variants permit unconstrained updates that induce catastrophic forgetting. To address these limitations, we propose a novel learnable wavelet activation that decomposes the activation function into low-frequency and high-frequency components to explicitly counter spectral bias. Furthermore, we employ dynamic wavelet injection to adaptively enhance plasticity for new tasks, alongside a regularization strategy to ensure the stability of previous learned knowledge. Theoretically, we provide rigorous mathematical guarantees for the proposed framework, proving the structural necessity of the hybrid wavelet architecture for efficient $L^2$ approximation and demonstrating that the decoupled learning rate mechanism successfully restores network plasticity for high-frequency information. Additionally, we provide a formal derivation of the loss-driven injection trigger mechanism to precisely guide the injection. Extensive empirical evaluations demonstrate that our approach maintains superior trainability and generalization throughout the learning process and achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse continual learning benchmarks.

</details>


### [111] [Robust data-driven discovery of fractional differential equations via weak formulations and Pareto-based subset selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12879)
*Pongpisit Thanasutives,Yoshinobu Kawahara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Weak-Pareto方法，利用伴随一致的弱形式公式化分数阶项，结合Pareto子集选择，从噪声数据中恢复分数阶偏微分方程，在多种基准测试中优于强形式方法和神经网络基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数阶偏微分方程描述非局部动力学，但从含噪数据中发现它们十分困难，原因在于分数阶微分会放大高频测量噪声，且导数的阶数未知。

Method: 采用伴随一致的弱形式将分数阶算子从测量场转移到光滑测试函数上，用平滑积分替代对噪声敏感的点态微分；对非线性项采用部分噪声抑制。系数通过分支感知的差分进化搜索分数阶，并配合岭回归拟合；支撑集大小在验证误差-复杂度肘点处选择。

Result: 发现固定线性右端项弱特征的方差在网格细化下消失，而点态分数阶特征的噪声放大随导数阶数增加。在分数阶对流扩散、反应扩散和Burgers基准上均能恢复简洁结构；在受控对比中，弱形式在所有乘性噪声水平下保持正确支撑集，而强形式在噪声引入后大多失败；弱库驱动噪声鲁棒性，连续阶Pareto搜索避免稠密固定字典的支撑选择失败；相比神经基线，运行时间大幅降低。

Conclusion: Weak-Pareto通过弱形式与连续阶Pareto搜索的组合，实现了对噪声鲁棒的分数阶偏微分方程结构发现，且比神经基线更高效更一致。

Abstract: Fractional partial differential equations describe nonlocal dynamics, but discovering them from noisy data is difficult because fractional differentiation amplifies high-frequency measurement noise and the derivative orders are unknown. We propose Weak-Pareto, which combines an adjoint-consistent weak formulation of fractional terms with Pareto-based subset selection over discrete term types and continuous fractional orders. For linear right-hand-side terms, the adjoint transfers fractional operators from measured fields to smooth test functions, replacing noise-sensitive pointwise differentiation with smoothing integration; for nonlinear terms, the noise-suppression effect is partial yet useful. Coefficients are fitted by ridge regression within a branch-aware differential-evolution search over the orders. The support size is then selected at the validation-error-complexity elbow. We show that the variance of fixed linear right-hand-side weak features vanishes under grid refinement, whereas noise amplification in pointwise fractional features increases with derivative order. Across fractional advection-diffusion, reaction-diffusion, and Burgers benchmarks, Weak-Pareto recovers parsimonious structures from clean and noisy measurements. In controlled advection-diffusion and Burgers comparisons, it retains the correct support at every tested multiplicative-noise level, whereas the unregularised strong-form counterpart largely fails once noise is introduced; this advantage persists under additive Gaussian noise. Ablations show that the weak library drives noise robustness and that continuous-order Pareto search avoids the support-selection failure of a dense fixed dictionary. On the advection-diffusion benchmark, Weak-Pareto yields more consistent operator recovery and substantially lower measured runtime than a contemporary neural baseline.

</details>


### [112] [Adaptive $k$ Nearest Neighbors Classifier via Granular Ball Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12903)
*Xiaoyu Lian,Shuyin Xia,Hongxuan He,Lifeng Shen,Guoyin Wang,Xinbo Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于粒球计算的自适应高效KNN方法（Adaptive-GBKNN），通过两阶段策略（训练阶段多粒度粒球划分、预测阶段加权距离定位与自适应邻域构建）动态确定k值，在准确性和效率上均优于现有KNN变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KNN算法中k值的选择对性能影响显著，传统方法通常依赖人工设定或交叉验证固定k值，难以适应不同数据分布，且效率较低。

Method: 两阶段方法：(1) 训练阶段，先对数据集进行粗粒度划分以降低粒球内数据分布复杂度，引入Fisher准则控制粒球分裂与停止，形成多粒度粒球表示；(2) 预测阶段，通过加权距离机制定位最近的粒球，在测试样本周围构建自适应邻域，有效k值由该邻域内实际样本数动态确定，利用最近粒球诱导的邻域提供更稳定的局部组信息以增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，该方法在准确性和效率两方面均优于现有KNN变体，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 基于粒球计算的自适应邻域构建能够动态确定k值，有效提升KNN在准确性和效率上的综合表现，并为KNN算法的自适应参数选择提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: The $k$-Nearest Neighbor~(KNN) algorithm is widely used across various tasks. The selection of the $k$ value is a key issue because it significantly impacts performance. In this paper, an adaptive and efficient KNN approach via granular-ball computing is proposed. The method consists of two stages. \textcolor{black}{In the training stage, the dataset is first coarsely partitioned to reduce the complexity of data distributions within a granular ball, and then the Fisher criterion is introduced to control ball splitting and stopping, yielding a multi-granularity granular ball representation. In the prediction stage, the nearest granular ball is first located through a weighted distance mechanism, and an adaptive neighborhood is then constructed around the test sample. The effective $k$ value is dynamically determined by the actual number of samples contained in this neighborhood. The neighborhood induced by the nearest granular ball provides more stable local group information, thereby improving robustness against noise and local perturbations.} Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms existing KNN variants across multiple datasets in terms of both accuracy and efficiency. The code has been open-sourced for reproducibility: https://github.com/lianxiaoyu724/Adaptive-GBKNN.

</details>


### [113] [EGRL: Edge generation-guided relation-aware learning for RNA-protein interaction prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12906)
*Danyu Li,Ling Zhou,Rubing Huang,Xian Zhong,Bin Zou,Kui Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EGRL框架，通过隐式元路径学习、多关系感知注意力机制、图生成器和多特征融合预测器，解决了现有GNN-based RPI预测方法在数据稀疏性和冷启动场景下的局限，在四个基准数据集上取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RNA-蛋白质相互作用（RPI）对细胞功能调控至关重要，传统湿实验检测代价高且耗时。虽然深度学习尤其是GNN方法提供了计算替代方案，但现有GNN方法依赖同构图或预定义元路径，难以处理数据稀疏性，且无法泛化到涉及未知分子的冷启动场景。

Method: EGRL框架包含四个关键组件：(1)隐式元路径学习，无需手工设计路径即可捕获关系语义；(2)多关系感知注意力机制，实现交互模式的自适应融合；(3)图生成器，预测潜在的"软"边以支持冷启动节点；(4)多特征融合预测器，进行最终交互评分。框架通过主任务损失和辅助生成器损失进行联合训练。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的综合评估显示，EGRL在整体性能上具有竞争力；在冷启动场景中表现尤为突出，在未知分子上达到AUROC 0.867和AUPR 0.861，相比之前最先进方法分别提升了8.6%的AUROC和5.0%的AUPR。

Conclusion: EGRL通过隐式元路径学习和图生成机制，有效解决了RPI预测中的数据稀疏和冷启动泛化问题，为RNA-蛋白质相互作用预测提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: RNA-Protein Interactions (RPIs) are critical for regulating cellular functions. While traditional wet-lab experiments for RPI detection are costly and time-consuming, Deep Learning (DL) methods provide an efficient computational alternative for RPI Prediction (RPIP). In particular, Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are promising, as they naturally model RPI networks. However, existing GNN-based methods often rely on homogeneous graphs or predefined meta-paths, which limit their ability to handle data sparsity and to generalize to cold-start scenarios involving unknown molecules. To address these limitations, we propose Edge Generation-guided Relation-aware Learning (EGRL), a novel framework with several key components: implicit meta-path learning to capture relational semantics without handcrafted paths; a multi-relation-aware attention mechanism for adaptive fusion of interaction patterns; a graph generator that predicts potential ("soft") edges to support cold-start nodes; and a multi-feature fusion predictor for final interaction scoring. EGRL is jointly trained with a primary task loss and an auxiliary generator loss. Comprehensive evaluations on four benchmark datasets demonstrate that EGRL achieves competitive overall performance. More importantly, it exhibits superior generalization in cold-start settings, achieving an Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic curve (AUROC) of 0.867 and an Area Under the Precision-Recall curve (AUPR) of 0.861 on unknown molecules, corresponding to improvements of 8.6% in AUROC and 5.0% in AUPR over prior state-of-the-art methods. The code will be released soon.

</details>


### [114] [Revisiting Overestimation Bias Problem of Q-learning: Settling Large Discrete Action Space via Action Intersection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12912)
*Pu Li,Tao Tan,Hong Xie,Xiaoyu Shi,Mingsheng Shang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"动作交集"(action intersection)策略来解决大动作空间下Q-learning的过估计偏差问题。通过让两个Q函数部分共享轨迹数据（共享时用耦合范式更新，不共享时用解耦范式更新），实现了半解耦设计，从而在过估计和欠估计之间获得可调的偏差范围，并在表格RL和深度RL实验中均取得了优于SOTA基线的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大动作空间中，Q-learning的过估计偏差问题加剧，且现有两大主流范式（耦合与解耦）各有瓶颈：耦合范式因随机性导致总是正偏差（偏好被高估的动作），解耦范式因随机性增大两个独立Q表对同一动作的估计差距而总是负偏差。作者希望找到一种简单而有效的策略来缓解这两类瓶颈。

Method: 提出动作交集（action intersection）策略，实现半解耦（semi-decoupling），包含两个设计：(1) 两个Q函数共享一定比例的轨迹数据；(2) 若数据样本被共享，则用耦合范式更新两个Q函数；否则用解耦范式更新。该策略具有两个关键性质：通过调整数据共享比例可使估计偏差在欠估计到过估计之间大范围变化；动作交集的大小可任意细化从而实现精细控制。

Result: 在深度RL实验中，所提方法大幅优于多个SOTA基线；在表格RL实验中，实验揭示了所提方法取得优异性能的内在原因，验证了动作交集策略能实现大范围且可细粒度调节的偏差控制。

Conclusion: 动作交集是一种简单而强大的策略，能在耦合与解耦范式之间平滑过渡，有效缓解大动作空间中Q-learning的过估计偏差瓶颈，为现有方法提供了统一的视角和改进空间。

Abstract: This paper considers the overestimation bias problem of Q-learning in the setting of a large action space, for the purpose of relieving the bottleneck of existing methods. We find that the large action space increases the randomness in Q-value estimation. The randomness makes two paradigms that drive the major literature on the overestimation problem have their own bottlenecks: the coupling paradigm, i.e., the optimal action and its Q-value are estimated with the same Q-function, always has a positive bias. This is because randomness leads to some actions having abnormally high estimated values than their true values, and the coupling methods prefer these actions. The decoupling paradigm, i.e., the optimal action and its Q-value are estimated with two independent Q-functions, always has a negative bias. This is because randomness increases the estimation gap between the two independent Q-tables for the same action. This paper shows that action intersection can be a simple yet powerful strategy to relieve these bottlenecks. The action intersection strategy enables semi-decoupling via two designs: (1) it allows two Q-functions to share a certain fraction of trajectory data; (2) if a data sample is shared, each Q-function is updated using the coupling paradigm; otherwise, using the decoupling paradigm. Two properties make the action intersection strategy powerful: (1) attaining a large bias range, i.e., varying the data sharing fraction, the estimation bias varies from underestimating to overestimating; (2) fine granularity: the action intersection size can be made arbitrarily finer to enable finer control. We consider two experiment settings, i.e., tabular and deep RL, deep RL experiments show that our method outperforms several SOTA baselines drastically; tabular experiments reveal why our method can achieve superior performance.

</details>


### [115] [Towards Socially Compliant Navigation in Deep Reinforcement Learning via Proxemics-Based Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12917)
*Takieddine Soualhi,Jacques Saraydaryan,Laetitia Matignon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于近体学（proxemics）理论的密集、可解释奖励函数，用于深度强化学习（DRL）的社交导航任务。该方法将每个人的个人空间建模为基于Hall近体学理论的径向高斯混合场，并在机器人视野内计算局部代价，在仿真中一致提升社交指标的同时保持导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DRL导航方法在拥挤环境中主要关注任务导向目标（如到达目的地），对社交合规目标（如尊重个人空间）的建模不足，导致机器人在人群中表现出不自然或不礼貌的行为。

Method: 1) 基于Hall近体学理论，将每个人的个人空间建模为径向高斯混合场；2) 计算机器人视野（FOV）内的机器人中心局部代价；3) 将该奖励函数集成到现有DRL导航方法中，在多种人群场景、奖励基线和人群密度下进行评估。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的奖励函数在社交指标上一致优于对比的奖励模型，同时导航性能与现有方法相比保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 该近体学奖励函数提供了一种密集、可解释的社交学习信号，能够在保持导航效率的同时提升机器人导航的社交合规性，为拥挤环境中的社交导航提供了有效奖励建模方案。

Abstract: Developing effective robot navigation methods in crowded environments is essential for real-world applications. Although recent deep reinforcement learning (DRL) methods have improved navigation performance in crowded environments, they often focus primarily on task-centric objectives and underrepresent social compliance objectives. In this paper, we introduce a novel proxemics-based reward formulation for DRL social navigation that provides a dense, interpretable social learning signal while maintaining navigation efficiency. Our approach models each human's personal space as a radial Gaussian-mixture field derived from Hall's proxemics theory and computes a robot-centric local cost over the robot's field of view. We integrate the proposed reward into established DRL navigation methods and evaluate it in simulation across multiple crowd scenarios, reward baselines, and crowd densities using both navigation metrics and social metrics. Results show that the proposed reward consistently improves social metrics in simulation while maintaining competitive navigation performance relative to the compared reward models.

</details>


### [116] [Momentum as Residual-Driven Multiplier Correction for Deep Learning Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12925)
*Zhixin Ren,Yau Lyu,Congrong Li,Liping Zhang,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AIM（ADMM启发式动量）框架，从残差-惩罚变量分裂的角度统一解释动量优化器，并据此设计RADAR优化器，在视觉、语言和强化学习任务中超越强自适应基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动量优化器在深度学习中广泛使用，但动量递归、更新几何和加速三者之间的关系尚未被完全理解。现有工作大多将动量视为启发式技巧，缺乏统一的理论框架来阐明其内在机制。

Method: 基于残差-惩罚变量分裂构建AIM框架，将动量解释为ADMM风格乘子更新的修正项，从而分离两个通常交织的机制：残差惩罚决定更新几何，目标相关子问题的近似决定加速形式。在该框架基础上提出RADAR优化器，结合相对论自适应几何、解耦残差校正和二阶动量滤波，并通过方差扰动的Lyapunov漂移分析建立随机收敛性。

Result: 在监督视觉学习、语言建模和强化学习等多项实验中，RADAR相较于强自适应优化器基线取得一致的性能提升，同时具备理论上的随机收敛保证。

Conclusion: AIM框架为理解动量优化器提供了统一理论视角，RADAR则验证了该框架指导实践的有效性，为设计新优化器开辟了系统化途径。

Abstract: Momentum-based optimizers are widely used in modern deep learning, yet the relations among momentum recursion, update geometry, and acceleration remain only partially understood. We develop an $\textbf{A}$DMM-$\textbf{I}$nspired $\textbf{M}$omentum (AIM) framework based on residual-penalty variable splitting, which interprets momentum as a multiplier-like correction driven by the splitting residual. AIM recovers the exponential moving average of gradients from an ADMM-style multiplier update and separates two mechanisms that are usually intertwined in practical optimizers: the residual penalty determines the update geometry, whereas the approximation of the objective-related subproblem determines the acceleration form. Building on AIM, we propose $\textbf{R}$elativistic $\textbf{A}$daptive gradient $\textbf{D}$escent with $\textbf{A}$ccelerated $\textbf{R}$esidual (RADAR), which combines relativistic adaptive geometry, decoupled residual correction, and second-order momentum filtering to improve the update direction and momentum estimation. We establish stochastic convergence through a variance-perturbed Lyapunov drift analysis. Experiments on supervised vision learning, language modeling, and reinforcement learning show that RADAR achieves consistent improvements over strong adaptive optimizer baselines.

</details>


### [117] [H-VAEP and H-xT: Valuing Offensive On-the-Ball Actions in Handball by Estimating Probabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12926)
*Julius Broermann,Oliver Müller,Michael Döring,Jochen Baumeister*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将Expected Threat (xT)和VAEP两种事件级动作价值评估框架系统性地适配到手球运动中，利用德国手球甲级联赛五个赛季的追踪数据开发了Handball-xT (H-xT)和Handball-VAEP (H-VAEP)，验证了其有效性与稳健性，并在手球运动员评估中展现了优越的区分度与直觉合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手球运动员评估依赖基础盒式统计或启发式指标，无法充分反映多人配合推进过程中的贡献。足球领域已被广泛采用的xT和VAEP框架尚未被适配到手球，缺乏能够衡量手球比赛中非得分动作（如传球、推进）价值的方法。

Method: 利用手球联赛五个赛季的追踪衍生事件数据，设计手球原生球场分区布局以构建H-xT，通过模拟实验验证其相比标准矩形网格更具鲁棒性；针对H-VAEP，定制其特征空间并选择上下文长度以限制球队身份信息泄露。

Result: 模拟实验表明H-xT的手球原生分区布局系统性地优于标准矩形网格；H-VAEP生成的球员评分异常稳定、具有高区分度和直觉合理性，并能突出组织推进型球员的价值。

Conclusion: H-xT和H-VAEP为职业手球俱乐部提供了更完善的球员评估工具，能够捕捉传统指标遗漏的配合推进价值；作者公开完整代码库以推动该方法的实际部署与进一步研究。

Abstract: Traditional player evaluation in professional handball relies on basic box-score metrics or heuristic indices, which fail to credit the multi-player build-up chain. While football (soccer) analytics has adopted Expected Threat (xT) and Valuing Actions by Estimating Probabilities (VAEP), these event-based action valuation frameworks have not yet been adapted to handball. In this paper, we present the first comprehensive adaptation and evaluation of xT and VAEP for handball, utilizing five seasons of tracking-derived event data from the Handball Bundesliga. We develop Handball-xT (H-xT) using a handball-native court zoning layout, demonstrating via simulations that it is systematically more robust than standard rectangular grids. We optimize Handball-VAEP (H-VAEP) by tailoring its feature space and selecting the context length to limit team-identity leakage. Our evaluation shows that H-VAEP yields exceptionally stable, discriminative, and intuitive player ratings that highlight build-up play. Finally, we release our complete code repository to help professional clubs deploy these models.

</details>


### [118] [Multi-perspective Imbalance-Conscious 6G Beamforming Optimization and Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12929)
*Chukwunonso Henry Nwokoye,Blessing Oluchi Iloka,Chikwue V. Umeugoji,Christopher Anene Egemba,Nnenna D. Duroha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了6G-IoT波束成形优化（6GBO）中的机器学习方法，比较了网络、环境、设备及视觉四类特征组的预测能力，并探索了无监督聚类方法（K-means、DBSCAN、层次聚类）及可解释性分析，发现网络特征预测能力最佳，部署环境和设备类型主导聚类结果，带宽、IoT传感器和移动性是关键全局特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G-IoT场景下波束成形优化面临高维、动态复杂的挑战，传统优化方法难以应对。为此，作者希望系统性地评估不同特征维度（网络、环境、设备、视觉）对波束成形优化的贡献，并探索无监督方法在场景聚类与可解释性方面的辅助潜力。

Method: 采用监督学习（比较四类特征组的预测能力）与无监督学习（K-means、DBSCAN、层次聚类）相结合的方法。评估指标包括召回率、F1-score、ROC-AUC（监督学习），以及Elbow、Silhouette score和Davies-Bouldin Index（无监督聚类评估）。此外进行了不平衡感知实验和全局特征重要性（可解释性）分析。

Result: 网络特征在预测6GBO方面优于设备、环境和视觉特征组（召回率、F1-score和ROC-AUC均更高）。无监督聚类结果表明部署环境和设备类型是影响聚类的主要因素，而非移动性属性。可解释性分析显示带宽、IoT传感器和移动性具有最高的全局特征重要性。

Conclusion: 网络特征应作为6GBO建模的首选输入，无监督聚类有助于理解场景结构的底层驱动因素。未来将引入深度学习和强化学习技术，以预测吞吐量/延迟或基于SNR增强等性能指标优化奖励函数。

Abstract: The study presents a systematic machine learning (ML) study of 6G-IoT beamforming optimization (6GBO) using supervised and unsupervised approaches. We compared the predictive power of network, environmental, device, and vision feature groups for 6GBO. Additionally, it addressed other unsupervised perspectives that can enhance 6GBO, including clustering network scenarios using methods such as K-means, DBSCAN, and hierarchical clustering. Several imbalance-aware experiments revealed that network features possess better prediction power than device, environmental, and vision feature groups, as evidenced by their recall, F1-score and ROC-AUC values. For unsupervised ML exploration (assessed using Elbow, Silhouette score, and Davies-Bouldin Index methods), the results indicate that the deployment environment and type of device primarily influence clustering, rather than mobility-based attributes. Furthermore, the explainability analysis showed that bandwidth, IoT sensors, and mobility possess higher global feature importance across the feature groups. In the future, we would apply deep and reinforcement learning techniques to predict throughput/latency or to optimize rewards determined by performance indicators like SNR enhancement

</details>


### [119] [Diagnosing JEPA World Models with Action-Conditioned Predictive Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12939)
*Guo An,Zijing Wu,Honghua Dong,Yuhao Yan,Zixuan Gui,Haochong Chen,Shanzhao Ruan,Xiang Wang,Yurong Ling,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双模拟（bisimulation）准则的诊断方法ACPC，用于量化联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）在视觉扰动下的鲁棒性。通过定义不变半径（IR）和分离率（SR）两个互补指标，论文证明了ACPC可以界定扰动引起的多步预测误差和规划器代价变化，并在四个视觉控制任务上验证了该诊断的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA架构虽然在隐空间中做预测而非像素空间，减少了对无关外观建模的压力，但仍无法保证对视觉扰动的鲁棒性——扰动仍可能改变编码表示并影响后续的动作条件预测。现有方法缺乏一个精确的标准来衡量哪些观测应被视为同一状态，因此需要一种系统的诊断工具来量化JEPA模型在视觉扰动下的脆弱性。

Method: 基于双模拟准则（两个观测只有在动作条件后果一致时才应被视为同一状态），作者提出行动条件预测一致性（ACPC）诊断指标。ACPC衡量一个干净历史与其视觉扰动视图在相同动作序列滚动后的发散程度。论文证明该发散度界定了扰动引起的多步预测误差和规划器代价的变化。在成对ACPC的基础上，进一步定义了两个互补指标：不变半径（IR）汇总干净-扰动滚动扩散程度，分离率（SR）检验不同状态在滚动后是否仍可区分。

Result: 在四个视觉控制任务上，成对ACPC能够预测扰动引起的预测误差和规划代价变化。在LeWM上，IR-SR筛选可以在任务间迁移，联合诊断在模糊和缩放扰动下依然有效。PLDM在不同架构下表现出类似的诊断趋势。

Conclusion: ACPC作为JEPA模型在视觉扰动下的鲁棒性诊断，能够有效量化扰动对预测和规划的影响，其IR和SR指标提供了互补的鲁棒性评估维度，且在不同架构和任务间具有泛化性。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) learn world models that predict in a compact latent space rather than in pixels, reducing the pressure to model nuisance appearance. Yet this provides no guarantee against visual perturbations: they can still alter the encoded representation and affect subsequent action-conditioned predictions. Bisimulation captures this requirement precisely: two observations should be treated as the same state only when their action-conditioned consequences agree. Guided by this criterion, we introduce Action-Conditioned Predictive Consistency (ACPC), a diagnostic that measures how far a clean history and a visually perturbed view of it diverge after being rolled forward under the same action sequence. We prove that this divergence bounds the perturbation-induced change in multi-step prediction error and planner cost. Building on pairwise ACPC, we define two complementary measures: the Invariance Radius (IR) summarizes clean-perturbed rollout spread, while the Separation Rate (SR) checks whether different states remain distinguishable after rollout. Experiments on four visual control tasks show that pairwise ACPC predicts perturbation-induced prediction and cost changes. On LeWM, the IR-SR screen transfers across tasks, and the joint diagnostic remains informative under blur and resize. PLDM exhibits similar diagnostic trends under a different architecture.

</details>


### [120] [CardioState-JEPA: Delay-Aware Cross-Modal Learning of a Shared Cardiac Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12944)
*Hamza Shafiq,Hung Manh Pham,Bin Zhu,Pan Zhou,Jun Hu,Aaqib Saeed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CardioState-JEPA是一个跨ECG、PPG、PCG三种心脏信号模态的心脏基础模型，基于生理感知的联合嵌入预测架构，将异构波形映射到共享Token空间并通过共享Transformer编码器学习，以预测掩蔽的心脏潜在状态为目标，利用延时对齐器处理不同心脏事件的时序偏移。在25个下游任务上，冻结编码器在PPG分类上平均提升8.2 AUROC点，PCG杂音检测提升18.8 AUROC点，ECG分类提升15.5 AUROC点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ECG、PPG和PCG提供同一心脏周期的互补视角，但现有心脏基础模型仅针对单一传感模态训练，未能利用跨传感器的共享生理信息。此外，同步多传感器记录稀缺，需要一种既能从丰富的单模态数据中学习，又能利用配对数据对齐模态间潜在心脏时间的方法。

Method: CardioState-JEPA采用生理感知的联合嵌入预测架构：将异构波形映射到公共Token空间，用单一共享Transformer编码器处理，通过预测掩蔽的潜在心脏状态进行学习。预训练目标聚焦于共享生理而非传感器特定的波形外观。针对电学、机械和血流动力学事件间的时序偏移，采用学习得到的延时对齐器在相应心脏时间进行跨模态预测。训练策略分为两步：先从丰富的单模态数据学习模态内结构，再利用配对数据在潜在心脏时间上对齐模态间关系。

Result: 在25个涵盖ECG、PPG、PCG的下游任务中，冻结编码器相对于最优自监督信号基线，在PPG分类上平均提升8.2 AUROC点，PCG杂音检测提升18.8 AUROC点，ECG分类提升15.5 AUROC点。此外，在多个ECG基准上匹配或超越了使用特权临床文本或监督标签训练的心脏模型。

Conclusion: 异构心脏信号能够相互监督训练一个统一的心脏生理基础模型，证明跨模态的共享生理信息是构建心脏基础模型的有效监督信号，优于单模态自监督方法和依赖特权信息的监督方法。

Abstract: Electrocardiography (ECG), photoplethysmography (PPG), and phonocardiography (PCG) provide complementary views of the same cardiac cycle, yet existing cardiac foundation models are trained for a single sensing modality, leaving the shared physiology across sensors unexploited. We introduce CardioState-JEPA, a cardiac foundation model to learn a single shared representation jointly across ECG, PPG, and PCG, built on a physiology-aware joint-embedding predictive architecture. The model maps heterogeneous waveforms into a common token space, processes them with a single shared Transformer encoder, and learns by predicting masked latent cardiac states, placing the pretraining target on shared physiology rather than sensor-specific waveform appearance. To handle the temporal offsets between electrical, mechanical, and hemodynamic events, cross-modal prediction uses a learned delay aligner that matches signals at the corresponding cardiac time. Because synchronized multi-sensor recordings are scarce, CardioState-JEPA first learns within-modality structure from abundant unimodal data and then uses paired data to align modalities in latent cardiac time. Evaluated as a frozen encoder across 25 downstream tasks spanning ECG, PPG, and PCG, our encoder improves average PPG classification by 8.2 AUROC points, PCG murmur detection by 18.8 AUROC points, and ECG classification by 15.5 AUROC points over the best self-supervised signal baseline and matches or exceeds cardiac models trained with privileged clinical text or supervised labels on several ECG benchmarks. These results establish that heterogeneous cardiac signals can mutually supervise a single foundation model of cardiac physiology.

</details>


### [121] [I-SDPO: Instance-Level Adaptive Self-Distillation Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12957)
*Yubo Zhang,Xinhong Ma,Zezhong Tan,Ziqiang Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出I-SDPO（实例级自适应自蒸馏策略优化），一种解决GRPO在无成功样本时缺乏有效相对信号问题的强化学习方法。I-SDPO根据每个输入实例的rollout组成功率做路由决策：全错的组使用特权自蒸馏目标，有成功的组保留GRPO。理论分析揭示了教师方向和奖励方向的对齐条件及有偏蒸馏权重的优化偏差下限，路由规则随成功率上升自动降低蒸馏率。在SciKnowEval四个科学领域均取得最优结果，平均mean@16精度从GRPO的56.67%提升至70.31%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO依赖组内奖励差异作为学习信号，但当rollout组内所有样本都错误时，无法获得有用的相对信号。特权自蒸馏虽能提供密集的token级监督弥补这一缺口，但若全程使用，会产生新的问题：教师是相对于奖励目标有偏的低方差替代，当策略已能产出成功轨迹后，持续模仿会与奖励改进方向相冲突。

Method: I-SDPO引入实例级的自适应路由机制：对每个输入实例只做一次决策，并将该决策共享给该实例的整个rollout组。具体地，当组内全部响应均错误时，使用特权自蒸馏目标（引导学生模仿教师）；当组内存在任何成功响应时，保留GRPO目标。同时通过局部理论分析刻画教师方向与奖励方向对齐的条件，证明非消失的有偏蒸馏权重会引入优化偏差下限。

Result: 在SciKnowEval基准上，I-SDPO在全部四个科学领域均取得最佳结果，平均mean@16精度从GRPO的56.67%提升至70.31%，最大单领域提升达18.24个百分点。

Conclusion: I-SDPO通过将教师依赖设计为能力相关的（capability-dependent）路由策略，仅在组间奖励信号无信息时启用蒸馏，既弥补了GRPO在无成功样本时的信号缺口，又避免了全程蒸馏造成的优化偏差。路由规则随成功率自然调整蒸馏率，无需手工设计的调度方案。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) learns from reward differences within a rollout group, but receives no useful relative signal when every sampled response is incorrect. Privileged self-distillation can fill this gap with dense token supervision, yet applying it throughout training creates a different failure mode: the teacher is a biased, low-variance surrogate for the reward objective, so persistent imitation can oppose reward-improving updates after the policy becomes capable of producing successful trajectories. We introduce I-SDPO (Instance-Level Adaptive Self-Distillation Policy Optimization), which treats teacher reliance as capability-dependent. I-SDPO makes one routing decision per input instance and shares it across that instance's rollout group: all-incorrect groups use a privileged self-distillation objective, whereas any-success groups remain intact for GRPO. This design uses imitation only where group-relative rewards are uninformative. A local analysis characterizes when teacher and reward directions align and shows that a non-vanishing biased distillation weight induces an optimization bias floor. The routing rule automatically reduces the expected distillation rate as success probability rises, withdrawing teacher influence without a hand-designed schedule. On SciKnowEval, I-SDPO obtains the best result in all four scientific domains and improves average mean@16 accuracy from 56.67% with GRPO to 70.31%, with a maximum domain gain of 18.24 points.

</details>


### [122] [The Objective Is the Bottleneck: Latent World Models Encode What Their Planners Cannot Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12959)
*Joyjeet Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现LeWorldModel在TwoRoom任务中长时程规划失败的瓶颈并非预测器退化，而是规划器的目标函数缺陷：交叉熵方法最小化平方潜距离，该度量在约120个环境单位后不单调，导致远离目标反而可能降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜世界模型通常通过预测质量来评判，规划在长时程失败时人们自然归因于预测器退化。但作者通过复现发现，真正的瓶颈可能是规划器的目标函数本身而非预测能力。

Method: 在TwoRoom任务上复现LeWorldModel，对比预测器精度与规划器行为；通过岭探针检验嵌入中的位置信息；仅替换规划目标函数（不改训练、不用GPU）进行消融实验；比较基于帧分离学到的距离头与位置探针的规划效果。

Result: 预测器在75步后误差仅为冻结假设的0.189，但规划器从不想象超过25步；平方潜距离与真实距离的相关性仅r=0.426，在约80单位处饱和、120单位后递减。用岭探针可从冻结嵌入恢复位置（R²=0.9922）。仅替换目标函数后，偏移100处的目标达成率从26.0%升至98.0%，与偏移25时的98.0%持平，且预算减至三分之一时仍达92.0%。位置探针预测距离更准（r=0.9897 vs 0.819）但计划效果反而更差。

Conclusion: 规划失败的根源是目标函数（成本函数）的单调性问题，而非预测器退化。最优成本不应追求最准确的度量（proximity），而应学习可达性（reachability）——这正是基于帧分离学习的距离头虽预测精度较低但规划效果更好的原因。该病理现象在作者发布权重及多个检查点中均存在，说明这属于方法层面的系统性问题。

Abstract: Latent world models are judged by how well they predict, so when planning fails at long horizons the natural reading is that the predictor degrades. On a reproduction of LeWorldModel on TwoRoom we show the binding constraint is the planner's objective instead.
  The predictor is not the limit: its imagined state seventy-five environment steps ahead is still only 0.189 as wrong as assuming the world froze, while the planner never imagines beyond twenty-five. The objective is. Cross-entropy-method planning minimises squared latent distance, which tracks true distance at r = 0.426, saturates by about eighty arena units and decreases beyond a hundred and twenty, so moving away from the goal can lower the cost. The information is present throughout: a ridge probe recovers position from the frozen embedding at R^2 0.9922.
  The pathology is the method's, not one reimplementation's. It is present in the authors' released weights, and across four checkpoints long-horizon success rank-orders exactly with metric quality and inversely with prediction accuracy.
  Replacing only the objective, with nothing retrained and no GPU, lifts goals reached at offset 100 from 26.0% to 98.0%, equals the 98.0% at offset 25, and reaches 92.0% under a third of the budget: planning stops depending on the horizon. The best cost is not the most accurate. A head learned from frame separation alone predicts spatial distance worse than a position probe (r = 0.819 against 0.9897) yet plans better, charging 24% more to cross the environment's dividing wall where squared latent distance charges 4% less. It has learned reachability, not proximity.

</details>


### [123] [Understanding Backdoor Vulnerabilities in Vertical Federated Learning: The Gap Between Research and Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12962)
*Ziqi Zhao,Jialin Lu,Junjie Shan,Junyuan Zhang,Shuya Yang,Ka-Ho Chow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地揭示了垂直联邦学习（VFL）中后门攻击研究在真实场景下的局限，指出现有研究依赖不切实际的先验知识和糟糕的评估实践，并提出BVBench基准框架以弥合研究与实际应用之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 垂直联邦学习（VFL）中，发起方和参与方之间存在信息不对称，这既带来了隐私保护的收益，也引入了安全风险。后门攻击是其中尤为严重的威胁，因为恶意参与方不仅能训练时投毒模型，还能在推理时激活后门操纵预测结果。尽管已有研究声称达到近完美的攻击成功率并提出了防御方法，但这些发现在真实条件下大多不成立，暴露出研究与实践之间的根本鸿沟。

Method: 本文首先系统性地审视VFL后门漏洞，指出现有方法忽视了关键的实际约束并依赖不现实的先验知识。其次，作者重新定义了符合现实约束的威胁模型，提出了实用的后门攻击流程，并构建了BVBench——一个以对抗基准为核心的评估框架，集成了多种先进的基线方法，支持公平、实用、全面的性能评估。

Result: BVBench提供了强有力的证据，表明当前对VFL后门风险的理解是脆弱的，现有攻击和防御方法在现实条件下均表现不佳。该基准揭示了文献中由不良评估实践掩盖的方法论缺陷，为后续研究提供了可复现的评估基础。

Conclusion: 本文的研究表明VFL后门攻击领域需要从理想化假设转向实践导向的研究范式。BVBench的提出为社区提供了公平、可比较的评估平台，有助于引导未来研究聚焦于真实世界的漏洞发现和更有意义的防御方案开发。

Abstract: Vertical Federated Learning (VFL) enables organizations holding complementary features of shared entities to collaborate and train models. In this setting, the initiator can withhold information about the learning task, while other contributors participate without exposing their local datasets, creating an asymmetric information structure aligned with growing privacy demands. However, this asymmetry is a double-edged sword. Among various threats, backdoor attacks are particularly concerning because VFL not only enables malicious contributors to poison the model during training, but also allows them to activate the backdoor at inference time to manipulate predictions. Although prior work has reported near-perfect attack success rates and proposed effective defenses, we find that most findings fail to hold under realistic conditions, exposing a fundamental gap between research and practice. In this paper, we present a systematic, practice-oriented study of backdoor vulnerabilities in VFL, revealing this gap in both methodological design and evaluation practices. We show that existing approaches overlook key practical constraints and therefore rely on unrealistic prior knowledge. Furthermore, these limitations have remained hidden due to poorly designed evaluation practices in the literature. To bridge this gap, we redefine threat models under realistic constraints, propose practical backdoor workflows, and introduce BVBench, a backdoor-centric benchmark that enables fair, practical, and comprehensive evaluation, preloaded with state-of-the-art baselines. BVBench provides strong evidence of the fragility of the current understanding of VFL backdoor risks and establishes a foundation for steering research toward uncovering practical vulnerabilities and developing more meaningful defenses.

</details>


### [124] [Comment on "Modeling rapid language learning by distilling Bayesian priors into artificial neural networks"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12974)
*Orr Well,Idan Tarshish,Nur Lan,Roni Katzir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文批判性审视了McCoy & Griffiths（2025）关于通过MAML将贝叶斯先验蒸馏进人工神经网络的主张。作者指出：(1) 在标准的先验解释下，MAML并未真正注入先验，只是初始化了网络权重；(2) 在更宽泛的解释下，系统视为贝叶斯学习器面临重大挑战；(3) 与真正的贝叶斯学习器相比，MAML模型过拟合且在未见数据上泛化差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: M&G声称通过MAML可以将贝叶斯先验有效蒸馏到ANN中，并将其与Yang & Piantadosi (2023)的贝叶斯学习器在形式语言学习能力上进行比较，声称表现相当。本文旨在检验这一主张的理论合理性和实证有效性，特别是质疑MAML是否真的蕴含了贝叶斯先验，以及MAML是否真的能达到贝叶斯学习的泛化效果。

Method: 本文采用理论分析结合实证评估的方法：(1) 首先从贝叶斯先验的标准定义出发，论证M&G的MAML过程不改变目标函数，因此不蕴含标准意义上的先验；(2) 探讨将整个系统视为贝叶斯学习器的更宽泛解释，并展示其面临的理论挑战；(3) 通过评估MAML对贝叶斯学习实证结果的近似程度，检验其泛化能力，与真正的贝叶斯学习器进行对比。

Result: (1) 在标准先验解释下，MAML只是有利地初始化了网络权重，目标函数未变，因此并未注入真正的贝叶斯先验；(2) 在更宽泛的解释下，将系统整体视为贝叶斯学习器面临非平凡的挑战；(3) 实证上，与真正的贝叶斯学习器不同，M&G的模型存在过拟合问题，对未见数据的泛化能力较差。

Conclusion: M&G关于MAML能够蒸馏贝叶斯先验到ANN中的主张缺乏充分的理论和实证支持。MAML并未在一标准意义上实现贝叶斯学习，其泛化表现也逊于真正的贝叶斯学习器。因此，需要对MAML作为贝叶斯先验蒸馏方法的有效性持谨慎态度。

Abstract: McCoy & Griffiths (2025, henceforth M&G) suggest that a Bayesian prior can be distilled into Artificial Neural Networks (ANNs) through Model-Agnostic Meta-Learning (MAML, Finn et al., 2017). They support this empirically by showing that meta-trained networks demonstrate formal language learning abilities comparable to Yang & Piantadosi (2023)'s Bayesian learner, significantly outperforming standard ANNs. We point out that under the standard interpretation of a prior, M&G's procedure does not actually instill one; it merely initializes network weights favorably, leaving the objective function unchanged. We then consider a more permissive interpretation, where the system as a whole can be seen as implementing a Bayesian learner even without an explicit prior in the objective. We show that this interpretation faces nontrivial challenges. Finally, we assess how well MAML approximates the empirical results of Bayesian learning, showing that unlike genuine Bayesian learners, M&G's model overfits and generalizes poorly to unseen data.

</details>


### [125] [Learning the Mathematical Property for Designing Low Mutual Coherence Binary Sensing Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12982)
*Rekha,Santosh Singh,S. K. Neogy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经网络构造二元感测矩阵的新方法，通过利用互相干性（mutual coherence）作为损失函数的数学约束，无需任何数据集和特定应用场景，从而大幅降低了计算成本，实现了信号的高效完美恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压缩感知中感测矩阵的构造是信号完美恢复的核心问题。传统的RIP、NSP和Spark约束均为NP-hard问题，计算上难以求解。现有学习式方法依赖大规模数据集且缺乏通用性，因此需要一种不依赖数据、不针对特定应用、且计算高效的感测矩阵构造方法。

Method: 采用神经网络框架构造二元感测矩阵。矩阵元素通过共享的底层规则生成，以低互相干性（low mutual coherence）为优化目标。创新点在于首次将数学性质（互相干性）直接用作神经网络的损失函数，且不依赖任何训练数据集或特定应用场景，从而保证了方法的通用性和鲁棒性。

Result: 所提出的方法成功构造出具有低互相干性的二元感测矩阵，能够有效实现稀疏信号的完美恢复。由于无需大规模训练数据集且架构简单，计算成本得到大幅降低，同时降低了存储需求，并提升了方法的泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文首次在文献中提出了将数学约束（互相干性）作为神经网络损失函数的感测矩阵构造方法，无需数据和特定应用，兼具通用性、鲁棒性与计算经济性，为压缩感知领域的感测矩阵设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: In this research work, we are constructing the sensing matrix, which is essential for the success of the compressive sensing technique. We have chosen a learning-based technique for the construction of the sensing matrix. The novelty and uniqueness of the proposed technique is that it does not use any data set and also does not use a specific application. It uses the mathematical property/constraint for the construction of the sensing matrix for the perfect recovery of the signal. The perfect recovery of signals is an old and still very challenging problem in real-world applications. In late 2000, compressive sensing became a popular mathematical tool for the perfect recovery of sparse signals. The core of the compressive technique is the construction of the sensing matrix, which satisfies certain special properties such as restricted isometry property (RIP), null space property (NSP), and spark property (SP). All these properties are NP-hard problems and hence computationally challenging to solve. For all practical purposes, the construction of the sensing matrix needs to achieve low mutual coherence to achieve the perfect recovery of the signals. We have used a neural network for the construction of the sensing matrix, and this framework constructs a binary sensing matrix with low mutual coherence. The entries in the matrix are generated through a shared underlying rule. The proposed architecture is simple and does not use large-scale training data sets. Such uniqueness and novelty bring a drastic reduction in computational cost, and also, for the first time in literature, the use of a mathematical property for defining the loss function. In this proposed research work, the mutual coherence property has been used in the neural network framework. Such a neural network framework brings generality, robustness, and reduces storage requirements.

</details>


### [126] [Balanced Adaptive Prototype Selection for Scalable TabPFN Inference on Large-Scale Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12989)
*Mahboobe Jadid,Melika Rezaye Garkani,Ali Mousavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BAPS框架，通过选择紧凑且信息保留的原型集来构建上下文，使预训练表格基础模型（TabPFN）无需修改或重训练即可扩展到百万行数据集，实现约1953倍的上下文压缩，同时在HIGGS和SUSY数据集上保持了较强的预测性能和可靠的校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练表格基础模型虽具有强大的预测能力，但其推理上下文长度有限，严重制约了在大规模数据集上的应用。现有方法无法在有限上下文内有效保留数据的关键信息，因此需要一种无需修改或重训练预训练模型的上下文构建机制。

Method: BAPS通过联合优化多个目标来选择信息保留的原型集，包括：代表性结构（representative structure）、信息性决策边界（informative decision boundaries）、局部密度（local density）、类别平衡（class balance）以及特征空间多样性（feature-space diversity）。该方法不需要修改或重训练预训练模型。

Result: 在包含百万行数据的HIGGS和SUSY数据集上，仅使用512个原型即可保持较强的预测性能和可靠的校准效果，实现了约1953倍的上下文压缩。所有实验在无GPU加速的Intel Core i7 CPU（16GB内存）环境中完成。

Conclusion: 有效的上下文构建是扩展预训练表格基础模型到百万级数据集的实际可行机制，BAPS证明了在不修改模型的前提下通过精心选择原型即可实现大规模数据的高效推理。

Abstract: Pretrained tabular foundation models have demonstrated strong predictive capability; however, their application to large-scale datasets remains constrained by the limited inference context. This paper introduces Balanced Adaptive Prototype Selection (BAPS), a framework for constructing compact, information-preserving contexts for scalable TabPFN inference. Without modifying or retraining the pretrained model, BAPS jointly preserves representative structure, informative decision boundaries, local density, class balance, and feature-space diversity. Experiments on the million-row HIGGS and SUSY datasets show that 512 prototypes retain strong predictive performance and reliable calibration, corresponding to an approximately 1,953-fold context compression. All experiments were conducted on an Intel Core i7 CPU with 16 GB RAM and no GPU acceleration. These findings establish effective context construction as a practical mechanism for extending pretrained tabular foundation models to million-scale datasets.

</details>


### [127] [Incremental Evaluation and Training in Relational Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13023)
*Jakub Peleška,Gustav Šír*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种增量式、多轮次的评估与训练范式，用于评估和提升关系深度学习（RDL）模型在时间演化数据库上的适应性和鲁棒性，并证明增量微调训练优于从头训练的昂贵基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RDL评估实践依赖于静态、单轮次的数据库快照，忽略了真实数据库随时间演化的连续性特征，导致现有基准无法捕捉模型性能在新数据不断积累过程中的变化，缺乏对模型时间鲁棒性的评估能力。

Method: 提出增量式多轮次（episode）评估与训练范式；在既有大规模数据集上分析数据演化与模型训练动态；设计了多种增量微调训练机制；引入新的时间评估指标，该指标优先关注近期未来的预测精度。

Result: 发现大多数预测任务中存在时间概念漂移；增量微调后的模型在时间评估指标上始终优于标准从头训练的昂贵基线。

Conclusion: 迁移学习在RDL场景中是可行且高效的，增量微调范式能更真实地反映并提升RDL模型在动态数据库环境中的表现。

Abstract: Relational Deep Learning (RDL) models multi-tabular databases as temporal heterogeneous graphs to enable end-to-end representation learning. However, prevailing RDL evaluation practices rely on static, single-episode dataset snapshots, overlooking the continuous, time-evolving nature of real-world databases. Consequently, current RDL benchmarks fail to capture how model performance changes as new data accumulates over time. To address this limitation, we introduce an incremental, multi-episode evaluation and training paradigm to assess and improve the temporal robustness and adaptability of state-of-the-art RDL models. Using established large-scale datasets, we examine data evolution and model training dynamics, demonstrating that temporal concept drifts occur in the majority of predictive tasks. We present multiple incremental training regimes for fine-tuning the models and demonstrate that transfer learning is both feasible and highly effective in the RDL setting. Alongside a new temporal evaluation metric that prioritizes near-future accuracy, we show that our incrementally fine-tuned models consistently outperform the standard, expensive, from-scratch trained baselines.

</details>


### [128] [On the global feature importance for interpretable and trustworthy heat demand forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13039)
*Milan Zdravković*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种ante-hoc可解释AI方法，用于评估区域供热系统中热需求预测机器学习模型的全局特征重要性。该方法结合Gradient Boosting的内在可解释性与Partial Dependence、Accumulated Local Effects和SHAP三种后验解释方法，所有方法均不依赖特征置换或扰动，以避免引入随机不真实数据带来的偏差。论文提供了结果讨论和互补性评估，并结合区域供热过程进行具体解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升区域供热系统智能控制中热需求预测机器学习模型的可解释性和可信度，从而应对社区标准合规、客户满意度及责任风险等挑战。

Method: 采用四种方法评估全局特征重要性：(1) Gradient Boosting方法的内在可解释性；(2) Partial Dependence（部分依赖图）；(3) Accumulated Local Effects（累积局部效应）；(4) SHAP。所有方法均避免使用特征置换或扰动策略，以防止因引入随机不真实数据实例而产生偏差。

Result: 论文提供了结果的讨论，包括各方法之间的互补性评估，以及针对区域供热过程背景下的具体解释。

Conclusion: 通过ante-hoc可解释AI方法论，在不使用特征扰动的前提下实现了对热需求预测模型全局特征重要性的可靠评估，增强了模型的可解释性与信任度。

Abstract: The paper introduces the ante-hoc Explainable AI methodology to assess the global feature importance of the Machine Learning models used for heat demand forecasting in intelligent control of District Heating Systems, with motivation to facilitate their interpretability and trustworthiness, hence addressing the challenges related to adherence to communal standards, customer satisfaction and liability risks. Methodology includes use of four different approaches, namely intrinsic interpretability of Gradient Boosting method and selected post-hoc methods, namely Partial Dependence, Accumulated Local Effects and SHAP. None of the selected methods assume feature permutation or perturbations which can introduce bias due to introduction of random unrealistic values of data instances. Discussion of results is provided, including the assessment of complementarities where applicable, with specific interpretations in context of the district heating processes.

</details>


### [129] [Latent On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13040)
*Guibin Zhang,Jiayang Lyu,Ran Sun,Xinlei Yu,Haoyu Zhao,Qibing Ren,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出潜变量在线自蒸馏（LOPD）方法，通过让教师的特权上下文自身从经验中端到端可学习（检索相关经验并组成连续潜token），取代传统OPSD方法中依赖人工设计的特权工件，在智能体工具使用和代码生成上超越现有方法，且学习效率显著更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线自蒸馏（OPSD）方法虽然利用特权自教师为学生提供密集监督，但高度依赖设计者预先指定的特权工件（如答案、反馈、技能或轨迹），限制了端到端可学习性和可扩展性，难以支撑持续自我改进的智能体进化需求。

Method: LOPD的核心创新在于将教师的特权上下文本身变为端到端可学习的：系统检索相关经验并将其组合为连续潜变量token来条件化自教师；学生则从任务和交互历史中生成轨迹，并在每个访问的前缀位置接收密集的token级监督。此外引入privileged-margin目标函数来稳定和调节潜上下文的学习。

Result: 实验表明LOPD在智能体工具使用和代码生成两个任务上均优于RLVR及OPSD、SDPO、Skill-SD等代表性OPSD方法；在学习效率上，以不到GRPO和Skill-SD 30%的推演预算即超越了它们。消融实验直接证实了使特权上下文可学习是实现这些增益的必要条件。

Conclusion: LOPD将智能体进化推向更可扩展和更自导向的范式，通过消除对人工指定特权工件的依赖，为智能体从经验中持续内化知识提供了一条更具端到端可学习性的路径。

Abstract: Enabling agents to learn from experience and internalize it into their policy has become a central problem in self-evolving AI. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) offers an effective pathway by using a privileged self-teacher to provide dense supervision on the student's own trajectories; however, existing methods still rely heavily on designer-specified privileged artifacts (e.g., answers, feedback, skills, or trajectories), limiting the end-to-end learnability and scalability required for continual self-improvement. In this work, we introduce Latent On-Policy Self-Distillation (LOPD), which, rather than proposing another hand-crafted OPSD variant with a newly prescribed form of privileged context, makes the teacher's privileged context itself learnable end-to-end from experience. Technically, LOPD retrieves relevant experiences and composes them into continuous latent tokens that condition a self-teacher, while the student generates trajectories from the task and interaction history and receives dense token-level supervision at every visited prefix. We further introduce a privileged-margin objective to stabilize and regulate the learning of latent context. Empirically, LOPD demonstrates (I) strong performance, outperforming RLVR and representative OPSD methods including OPSD, SDPO, and Skill-SD across both agentic tool use and code generation; and (II) high learning efficiency, surpassing GRPO and Skill-SD with less than 30% of their rollout budget. Ablation studies further provide direct evidence that making privileged context learnable is necessary for realizing these gains. Together, these results position LOPD as a step toward a more scalable and self-directed paradigm for agent evolution.

</details>


### [130] [A Multispectral Framework for the Detection of Calcium Carbide-Induced Ripening and Shelf-Life Estimation in Climacteric Fruits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13073)
*Gurbhit Chaurakoti,Harshit Kumar,Hani Kumar,Anurag Singh,Ram Asrey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于AS7265x光谱三合一传感器的非侵入式多光谱框架，通过分析芒果和香蕉在410-940nm范围内18个离散波长的光谱特征，结合PCA降维和XGBoost算法，实现对自然成熟、乙烯利催熟和碳化钙催熟水果的区分，并估算其成熟度百分比和剩余货架期。芒果样本分类准确率达95%，香蕉为81%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非法使用工业级碳化钙（CaC2）催熟芒果和香蕉等呼吸跃变型水果是常见做法，但会留下砷和磷的微量残留，对健康构成严重风险。现有的检测方法可能存在侵入性或操作复杂等问题，因此需要一种非侵入式、高效且可靠的手段来区分安全催熟水果与碳化钙催熟水果。

Method: 利用AS7265x光谱三合一传感器在410-940nm可见-近红外范围内采集芒果和香蕉的18个离散波长光谱数据；针对碳化钙处理样品在可见光区域表现出的更剧烈光谱强度下降（对应叶绿素加速降解和类胡萝卜素发育），设计特征工程策略，整合方法间光谱方差、特定波长强度比以及温湿度等环境参数；采用PCA降维（前5-7个主成分保留>90%光谱方差）；训练三个独立的基于XGBoost的学习算法，分别用于催熟方法分类、剩余货架期估算和成熟度进度估算。

Result: 芒果样本的分类准确率为95%，碳化钙类别召回率为0.67；香蕉模型准确率为81%，碳化钙类别召回率为0.74。该框架能够有效区分不同催熟方式并定量估算成熟度和货架期。

Conclusion: 所提出的非侵入式多光谱框架结合仪器和数据驱动方法，有效证明了其在食品安全检测领域的应用潜力，可用于区分安全催熟与碳化钙催熟的水果，并估算其成熟进度和剩余货架期。

Abstract: Significant health risks are associated with the illegal, yet commonly practiced use of industrial-grade Calcium Carbide (CaC2) for ripening climacteric fruits like mango and banana, which leaves behind trace residues of arsenic and phosphorus. To address this, the proposed study explores a novel, non-invasive multispectral framework for distinguishing safely ripened fruits (naturally ripened and ethephon-induced) from calcium carbide-ripened samples, while also estimating their ripening progression (in percentage) and remaining shelf life (in days). The spectral profiles of mango (Mangifera indica) and banana (Musa acuminata) at 18 discrete wavelengths in the visible-near infrared (NIR) range (410 nm - 940 nm) are studied using the AS7265x spectral triad sensor. CaC2-treated samples exhibit sharper spectral intensity drops in the visible region, consistent with accelerated chlorophyll degradation and carotenoid development. To characterize these physiological changes, the feature engineering strategy integrates inter-method spectral variance, intensity ratios at distinct wavelengths, and environmental parameters including temperature and humidity. Dimensionality reduction using Principal Component Analysis (PCA) retains >90% of spectral variance within the first 5-7 components. The resulting feature set is used to train three independent eXtreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost)-based learning algorithms for ripening method classification, along with quantitative estimation of remaining shelf life and ripening progression. A classification accuracy of 95% along with carbide class recall of 0.67 is observed for mango samples, while the model achieves an accuracy of 81% and carbide class recall of 0.74 for banana. This instrumentation and data-driven approach demonstrates the effectiveness of the proposed non-invasive framework.

</details>


### [131] [Learning Discrete Decisions for MIPs with Constraint-Aware Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13079)
*Vincenzo Di Vito,Mehdi Taghizadeh,Deepjyoti Deka,Kaarthik Sundar,Ferdinando Fioretto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图生成扩散模型的学习方法CGD，用于近似求解混合整数优化问题，通过在学习过程中集成无需训练的可行性投影算子，将中间样本引导至可行集，在保持可行性的同时实现高达425倍的求解加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合整数优化问题需要同时确定离散和连续决策并满足复杂的组合约束，计算上极具挑战性。传统数值求解器在处理大规模MINLP时效率低下，亟需一种能够高效近似求解且保证可行性的学习方法。

Method: CGD框架包含两个核心组件：1）基于图的生成扩散模型学习混合整数优化问题的离散决策部分；2）在逆向扩散过程中直接集成无需训练的可行性投影算子，将中间样本持续引导向可行集。生成离散决策后，剩余问题退化为连续优化问题，可借助现有数值方法高效求解。该框架是问题无关的，可通过合适的投影算子适配广泛的混合整数优化问题。

Result: 在ACOPF的最优输电切换和离散组合投资优化两个任务上，CGD相比学习基线在可行性和解质量上均有显著提升，相比最先进的MINLP数值求解器实现最高425倍的加速。

Conclusion: CGD通过将扩散生成模型与可行性投影机制相结合，为混合整数优化问题提供了一种高效且问题无关的近似求解范式，在保持解可行性的同时大幅提升了求解速度，展示了学习驱动方法在组合优化领域的巨大潜力。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel learning-based approach to approximately solve instances of mixed-integer optimization problems. These problems are computationally challenging, as they require jointly determining discrete and continuous decisions while satisfying complex combinatorial constraints. The proposed method relies on a graph-based generative diffusion model that learns the discrete component of mixed-integer optimization problems while integrating a training-free feasibility projection operator directly into the reverse diffusion process to steer intermediate samples toward the feasible set throughout generation. Once the discrete decisions are generated, the remaining optimization reduces to a continuous problem that can be solved efficiently (relative to the original problem) using existing numerical methods. The resulting framework named Constrained Graph Diffusion (CGD), is problem-agnostic and can accommodate a broad class of mixed-integer optimization problems through suitable projection operators. We evaluate CGD on optimal transmission switching for ACOPF and discrete portfolio optimization, demonstrating substantial improvements in feasibility and solution quality over learning-based baselines while achieving speedups of up to $425\times$ over state-of-the-art numerical solvers for MINLPs.

</details>


### [132] [FlowLOB: Efficient and Controllable Limit Order Book Generation with Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13096)
*Zhuohan Wang,Andreea Bacalum,Ollie Olby,Carmine Ventre,Namid Stillman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowLOB是一个基于flow-matching的条件生成模型，用于模拟限价订单簿（LOB）轨迹。它训练于港交所多个标的在三种采样频率下的tick相对表示数据，仅需10步ODE求解器即可达到最优质量，相比扩散模型大幅减少计算开销，在真实性和可控性上优于现有基模型，并能零样本迁移到未见过的标的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LIMIT ORDER BOOK（LOB）模拟器难以同时满足四个关键需求：真实的市场动态、计算高效的采样、可控的场景生成，以及超越训练标的的泛化能力。现有的基于智能体（agent-based）和深度生成（deep generative）的模拟器只能部分满足这些属性，因此需要一种能够兼顾全部四项能力的统一框架。

Method: FlowLOB采用条件flow-matching生成器，在tick相对表示下，对港交所（HKEX）多个标的在0.1s、1s和10s三个采样频率下进行训练。由于flow和扩散模型共享统一公式框架，作者使用完全相同的数据、架构和预算训练两者，并通过相同的固定步长ODE求解器采样，形成受控对比。FlowLOB在仅10步ODE求解时达到最佳质量，而扩散模型需要更多函数评估才能接近相同保真度。

Result: 在10步ODE求解器的高效工作点下，FlowLOB在大多数分布度量指标上优于四个基线模型（两个学习方法、两个agent-based模型）在两个较细采样频率下的表现。分布检验显示FlowLOB在大多数测试场景中满足反事实可控性标准。真实性和控制效果均能零样本迁移到留出（held-out）标的。此外还完成了网络架构和学习率的消融实验。

Conclusion: FlowLOB证明了flow-matching方法在LOB轨迹生成中的优势：相比扩散模型显著减少采样计算开销（10步vs更多步），同时保持更高的真实性和可控性，并且具有跨标的零样本泛化能力，为实践者在高效、真实、可控的LOB模拟器方面提供了一个更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Limit order book (LOB) simulators are most useful to practitioners when they combine realistic market dynamics, computationally efficient sampling, controllable scenario generation, and the ability to generalize beyond the instruments seen during training---properties that existing agent-based and deep generative simulators provide only partially. We present \textbf{FlowLOB}, a conditional \textbf{flow}-matching generator of \textbf{LOB} trajectories, trained on multiple Hong Kong Exchange (HKEX) symbols at three sampling frequencies ($0.1$s, $1$s, $10$s) in tick-relative representation that transfers to unseen instruments. Because flow and diffusion models admit a common formulation, we train both with identical data, architecture, and budget, and sample both through the same fixed-step ODE solvers, yielding a controlled comparison of sampling efficiency and fidelity. Flow matching attains its best quality with only $10$ ODE-solver steps, whereas diffusion needs many more function evaluations to approach the same fidelity. At this efficient operating point, FlowLOB improves realism over baselines, two learned and two agent-based models, in most distributional metrics at the two finer sampling frequencies. We evaluate counterfactual controllability with a distributional test that asks whether changing a scenario condition moves the generated statistic toward the corresponding real tail regime; FlowLOB satisfies this criterion in most tested settings. Both realism and control effects transfer zero-shot on a held-out symbol. We additionally conduct ablation studies on the network architecture and the learning rate.

</details>


### [133] [Branch and Bound for Relational Verification of Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13118)
*Kota Fukuda,Zhenya Zhang,Guanqin Zhang,Jianjun Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分支定界（BaB）的框架SaBRe，用于验证神经网络的关系规约（如全局鲁棒性）。与传统方法不同，SaBRe分裂关系神经元而非单个神经元，并基于对偶公式设计关系神经元选择策略，以高效选出最大化分裂收益的最优神经元。在817个验证问题上（ACAS Xu、MNIST-F、MNIST-C、CIFAR、GTSRB），SaBRe在求解数量和验证效率上均优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在安全关键的CPS中广泛应用，验证其关系规约（如全局鲁棒性）至关重要。与局部鲁棒性等简单迹属性不同，关系规约需要推理多次网络推理之间的关系，带来了显著的技术挑战。现有基于凸过逼近的抽象方法本质上不完备，可能产生误报，因此亟需有效的抽象精化机制。

Method: 提出分支定界（BaB）框架，通过迭代分裂问题直至所有子问题被验证。核心创新包括：(1) 分裂关系神经元而非个体神经元（区别于已有工作）；(2) 基于验证问题对偶公式设计关系神经元选择策略，高效选取（最可能的）最优关系神经元以最大化分裂带来的精化收益。

Result: 在包含ACAS Xu、MNIST-F、MNIST-C、CIFAR和GTSRB的817个验证问题上进行评测，SaBRe在求解实例数量和验证效率两方面均优于不同基线方法，证明了所提技术的有效性。

Conclusion: SaBRe通过分裂关系神经元和基于对偶公式的选择策略，显著提升了神经网络关系规约验证的完备性和效率，为安全关键的CPS中AI组件的验证提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Verification of neural networks against relational specifications, such as global robustness, is crucial for safety-critical applications of cyber-physical systems (CPS), given their increasing adoption of AI components. Compared to simple trace properties (e.g., local robustness), verifying relational specifications requires reasoning about the relationship between multiple network inferences, which brings significant technical challenges. Existing research has explored abstraction techniques based on sound and convex over-approximation of neural network outputs; however, since these approaches are inherently incomplete and may raise false alarms, they further underscore the need of effective abstraction refinement.
  In this paper, we propose a branch-and-bound (BaB) framework to mitigate the issue, which iteratively splits the problem until all sub-problems are verified. Specifically, our BaB framework features splitting of relational neurons rather than individual neurons as prior works do, and as the core of our technique, we devise a relational neuron selection strategy based on the dual formulation of the verification problem, which allows us to efficiently select the (most likely) optimal relational neuron that maximizes the refinement brought by problem splitting. We evaluate SaBRe on 817 verification problems across ACAS Xu, MNIST-F, MNIST-C, CIFAR and GTSRB. The results show that SaBRe outperforms different baseline approaches, in terms of the number of solved instances and verification efficiency, which demonstrates the effectiveness of our proposed techniques.

</details>


### [134] [ProME: Prototype-Margin Environments with Repair-Aware Selection for Group-Robust Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13190)
*Qianqian Wang,Yunshan Li,Dawei Huang,Wenwu Gong,Lili Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ProME方法解决无训练组标签的群鲁棒学习问题，通过在原型边缘中位数处分割构造平衡环境，并在群标注验证数据上拟合组平衡线性头来评估候选预测器，从而将环境推断和表示选择与部署预测器对齐，在理论上给出最坏风险界并在实验中取得最优的平均最差组准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无训练组标签的群鲁棒学习方法通常在推断环境时使用独立的参考模型，且选择表示后才拟合部署所用的分类器，导致环境推断和表示选择与部署预测器不对齐，影响模型在稀有子群体上的表现。

Method: 将无训练组标签的群鲁棒学习形式化为ERAS问题，提出ProME方法：沿训练轨迹在原型边缘的中位数处分割，构造近似平衡的环境；在群标注验证数据上拟合组平衡线性头，根据验证最差组准确率对候选预测器进行排序。

Result: 理论上对固定预测器和划分给出了推断环境下的最坏风险界，并在显式对齐条件下证明该界可迁移到oracle组；实验中证明原型边缘能丰富捷径冲突样本，分类器修复能重塑候选评估，ProME在相同组标签访问条件下取得最高的平均最差组准确率。

Conclusion: 通过将环境推断和表示选择与部署预测器对齐，ProME有效提升了无训练组标签场景下的群鲁棒性能，理论界与实验验证均支持该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Group-robust learning is crucial for maintaining accuracy on rare subpopulations when training-group labels are unavailable. However, existing methods often infer environments from a separate reference model and select representations before fitting the classifier used at deployment, leaving both decisions misaligned with the deployed predictor. In this work, we formulate group robustness without training-group labels as the endogenous environments with repair-aware selection (ERAS) problem, and propose ProME (Prototype-Margin Environments) to align both decisions with the deployed predictor. ProME splits prototype margins at their median to construct approximately balanced environments along the training trajectory, and fits a group-balanced linear head on group-annotated validation data to rank the resulting predictors by validation worst-group accuracy. We theoretically bound the worst risk across the inferred environments for a fixed predictor and partition, showing that this bound transfers to the oracle groups under an explicit alignment condition. Extensive experiments show that prototype margins enrich shortcut-conflicting examples, classifier repair reshapes candidate evaluation, and ProME achieves the highest average worst-group accuracy among the compared methods with the same group-label access.

</details>


### [135] [Beyond Simulated Benchmarks: Evaluating Motion Representations for Fall Detection Under Real-World Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13197)
*Timilehin B. Aderinola,Ilaria D'Ascanio,Luca Palmerini,Lorenzo Chiari,Jochen Klenk,Clemens Becker,Brian Caulfield,Georgiana Ifrim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了可穿戴跌倒检测中不同运动表示方法在真实世界数据稀缺条件下的表现，发现高参数化的核方法和基础模型在模拟数据上表现优异但在真实数据和域迁移下严重退化，而结合物理冲击描述符的符号表示在域迁移下退化最小，强调了超越模拟基准评估的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界跌倒数据极为稀缺（收集100次跌倒约需100,000天监测），导致标注数据严重不足，而现有研究多依赖模拟数据集训练模型，往往实验室表现优异但真实泛化能力有限。因此需要系统评估不同运动表示方法在真实数据稀缺条件下的鲁棒性。

Method: 使用加速度计信号，系统比较四种运动表示方法：基于区间的表示、基于核的表示、符号表示和基础模型（foundation model）表示。同时提出一种轻量级可解释的符号表示方法，将短运动片段转换为符号句子并增强物理冲击描述符。实验使用FallAllD模拟跌倒数据集和FARSEEING临床验证的真实跌倒数据集，通过交叉验证、受控数据稀缺和跨数据集迁移进行评估。

Result: 高参数化的核方法和基础模型在模拟数据上表现优异，但在数据稀缺和域迁移下严重退化；基于区间的表示在绝对真实世界性能上最强；而结合物理冲击描述符的符号表示在域迁移下降解最小，且在极端稀缺下仍保持检测灵敏度，但精度较低。

Conclusion: 表示方法的选择对于可部署的跌倒检测系统至关重要，需要超越模拟基准进行评估。物理约束的符号表示在真实世界数据稀缺和域迁移条件下展现出更强的鲁棒性，是实际部署中值得关注的方案。

Abstract: Falls are a major health concern for older adults, and wearable sensors have been widely explored for detecting falls and enabling timely intervention. However, real-world falls are extremely rare: collecting 100 of them requires an estimated 100,000 days of monitoring, resulting in severely limited labelled data for training machine learning models. Consequently, many approaches rely on simulated datasets, often reporting high laboratory performance but limited real-world generalisation. We present a systematic evaluation of motion representations for wearable fall detection under real-world data scarcity. Using accelerometer signals, we compare interval-based, kernel-based, symbolic, and foundation model representations. As an interpretable baseline, we additionally investigate a lightweight symbolic representation that converts short motion segments into symbolic sentences augmented with physically-grounded impact descriptors. Experiments use FallAllD, a simulated falls dataset, and FARSEEING, a clinically verified real-world falls dataset. Through cross-validation, controlled data scarcity, and cross-dataset transfer, we examine how representation choices affect robustness under realistic deployment. Our results reveal that highly parameterised kernel and foundation models excel on simulated data but degrade severely under both data scarcity and domain shift. Although the interval-based representation achieves the strongest absolute real-world performance, augmenting a symbolic representation with physically-grounded impact descriptors yields the smallest degradation under domain shift and retains detection sensitivity under extreme scarcity, albeit at lower precision. These findings highlight the importance of evaluating beyond simulated benchmarks and show that representation choice is critical for deployable fall detection given the scarcity of real-world data.

</details>


### [136] [TANGCO: Learning Topology-Aware Capacity Allocation for Overload-driven Cascading Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13212)
*Orkun Irsoy,Leman Akoglu,Osman Yagan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TANGCO，一种基于图神经网络策略的容量分配方法，用于在局部负载再分配下抵抗级联故障。该方法通过级联模拟器结合策略梯度学习训练，在五个合成图族和五个真实网络（电力、道路、航空、互联网）上优于四种手工启发式方法，且训练可迁移、扩展近线性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络化系统（如电网、交通网络、云集群）中的节点容量有限，负载超限会导致级联故障。如何分配固定容量预算以抵抗级联是该问题的核心挑战，但现有最优分配未知，且故障-生存目标函数不可微、分段常数，精确优化和梯度方法均不适用。

Method: TANGCO使用图神经网络作为策略网络，通过级联模拟器进行策略梯度学习，并引入启发式锚点辅助训练。还提出预训练变体TANGCO^{pre}在合成图上预训练后可迁移到未见过的真实网络，无需逐目标训练。

Result: 在450个合成实例中全部优于最优手工启发式，在45个真实网络条件中40个优于最佳启发式，鲁棒性提升范围1.6%至246%。策略可迁移到同族未见图及部分相关拓扑，预训练版本匹配逐网络训练效果，训练规模近线性扩展，部署成本与启发式持平。Free-vector变体仅略优于启发式，证明图表示是关键增益来源。

Conclusion: 对学习到的分配进行深入分析，识别出局部风险何时充分的条件，进而改进了闭式启发式方法，同时揭示了拓扑感知学习策略仍然必要的机制场景。

Abstract: Networked systems, from power grids to traffic networks and cloud clusters, carry loads across nodes with limited capacity. A node whose load exceeds its capacity fails and sheds its load onto its neighbors, which can trigger a system-wide cascade. We study how to allocate a fixed capacity budget across nodes to resist these cascades under local load redistribution. The problem is difficult because no optimal allocation is known, and the fail-or-survive objective is non-differentiable and piecewise constant, so exact and gradient-based optimization methods do not directly apply. We introduce TANGCO (Topology-Aware Neural Graph-Guided Capacity Optimization), which uses a graph neural network policy trained through the cascade simulator with policy-gradient learning and a heuristic anchor. We evaluate TANGCO on five synthetic graph families and five real networks spanning power, road, air, and Internet topologies. The learned policy improves on the best of four hand-designed heuristics in all 450 synthetic instances and in 40 of 45 real-network conditions, with robustness gains ranging from 1.6% to 246%. The learned policies transfer to unseen graphs within a family and partially across related topologies, and TANGCO$^{pre}$, pre-trained on synthetic graphs, matches per-network training on unseen real networks. Training scales near-linearly with graph size, and TANGCO$^{pre}$ allocates on a new network with no per-target training, matching the deployment cost of a hand-designed heuristic. Free-vector variants without the GNN, stay close to the heuristics, so the graph representation carries the gain beyond numerical search. Finally, analysis of the learned allocations identifies when local risk is sufficient, leads to an improved closed-form heuristic, and reveals the regimes where a topology-aware learned policy remains necessary.

</details>


### [137] [History-informed Lagrangian Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13215)
*Tianshuo Zhang,Xianglei Xing,Wenzhe Zhai,Jia Gao,He Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出History-informed Lagrangian Neural Networks (HiLNN)，从纯位置观测中推断隐藏速度和系统参数，通过循环编码器提取历史上下文来重构初始速度并自适应调制拉格朗日系统的质量矩阵、势能和阻尼系数，结合可微RK4展开实现端到端多步训练，在保守、耗散和异构变参数系统中均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期机械系统演化预测是一大难题，因为隐藏速度和轨迹特定的物理属性需同时推断。现有物理引导神经网络（如LNN）虽然保证物理合理性，但通常需要完整状态输入且缺乏对变化系统参数的适应性。

Method: HiLNN采用循环编码器从时间位置序列中提取潜在上下文，该上下文既能重构未观测的初始速度，又能自适应调制结构化拉格朗日系统的质量矩阵、势能和阻尼系数。整个管线通过可微RK4 rollout方案在多重轨迹监督和能量一致性正则化下端到端优化。

Result: 在保守、耗散和异构变参数系统上的实证评估表明，HiLNN相较现有基线在长期预测精度上表现更优，同时能维持精确的能量分布。

Conclusion: HiLNN通过历史感知的潜在上下文编码，有效突破了传统LNN对完整状态输入和固定参数的依赖，实现了从纯位置观测到长期物理一致预测的统一框架。

Abstract: Forecasting the long-horizon evolution of mechanical systems from position-only observations is a pivotal yet difficult task, as hidden velocities and trajectory-specific physical properties must be inferred simultaneously. Although physics-guided neural networks like Lagrangian Neural Networks (LNNs) guarantee physical plausibility, they generally require complete state inputs and lack adaptability to changing system parameters. To break these limitations, we introduce History-informed Lagrangian Neural Networks (HiLNN). Grounded in the insight that temporal position sequences implicitly encode underlying dynamics, HiLNN employs a recurrent encoder to extract a latent context from history. This context not only reconstructs the unobserved initial velocity but also adaptively modulates the mass matrix, potential energy, and damping coefficients of a structured Lagrangian system. By leveraging a differentiable RK4 rollout scheme, the entire pipeline is optimized end-to-end under multi-step trajectory supervision and energy-consistency regularization. Empirical evaluations across conservative, dissipative, and heterogeneous variable-parameter systems show that HiLNN delivers superior long-term prediction accuracy and maintains precise energy profiles compared to state-of-the-art baselines. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/yingtian22/History-informed-LNN.

</details>


### [138] [Knowledge-guided Pattern Discovery via Coupled Tensor Factorizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13234)
*Gaute Johannessen,Geert Roelof van der Ploeg,Evrim Acar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种知识引导的张量分解方法，通过线性耦合将真实多模态数据与计算模型生成的模拟数据联合分析，在代谢组学数据上验证了该方法能提升模式发现性能并揭示数据与计算模型之间的潜在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统（如人类代谢组、大脑）产生的多路数据（multiway data）可通过张量分解提取可解释模式，但现有张量分解方法主要是纯数据驱动的，忽略了计算模型所蕴含的丰富先验知识。作者希望将真实数据和计算模型结合起来，利用模型模拟数据作为先验引导，提升模式发现的准确性。

Method: 提出知识引导的耦合张量分解方法（knowledge-guided coupled tensor factorizations with linear coupling），将真实实验数据与计算模型生成的模拟数据通过线性耦合约束联合进行张量分解，使得分解过程同时受到真实数据观测和模型先验信息的引导。

Result: 在真实代谢组学测量数据上的实验表明，用模拟数据引导含噪真实数据的分析能够提升模式发现的性能，同时还能揭示真实数据与计算模型之间的潜在不一致性（potential discrepancies）。

Conclusion: 融合数据驱动与模型驱动的知识引导张量分解范式是可行的，能够同时改善模式挖掘质量并为数据-模型一致性提供诊断信息，为理解复杂系统提供了新的分析框架。

Abstract: In order to understand complex systems such as the human metabolome or human brain, different sensing technologies are used, generating complex data. These datasets are often multiway, i.e., with more than two axes of variation such as a subjects by metabolites by time array. While tensor factorizations have successfully revealed interpretable patterns from such complex data, they have so far been mainly data-driven. On the other hand, there is more to data -- there are computational models (of these systems), which are rich sources of prior information. In this paper, we introduce a knowledge-guided approach that brings together data and computational models by jointly analyzing real data and simulated data (generated using a computational model) using coupled tensor factorizations with linear coupling. Our experiments on real metabolomics measurements demonstrate that guiding the analysis of such noisy data with simulated data improves the pattern discovery performance while also revealing potential discrepancies between data and computational models.

</details>


### [139] [Novel Knowledge-Guided Generative Methods for Synthetic Transcriptomic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13256)
