*Yicong Jiang,Feihu Huang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类基于Moreau包络的无Hessian多目标双层优化算法（MOMEHA）及其动量变体（MB-MOMEHA），用于解决下层级问题非凸的多目标双层学习问题。该方法通过Moreau包络将原问题转化为带包络约束的多目标单层优化，并利用光滑加权Tchebycheff标量化保持单循环和无Hessian的计算优势。理论部分给出了确定性和随机两种设定下的收敛性保证，实验在少样本元学习和神经架构搜索任务上验证了方法的有效性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标双层优化在AI领域（如自动学习和多任务元学习）有广泛应用，但现有方法依赖（强）凸的下层问题。实际中这些多目标双层学习问题通常是非凸的，特别是下层问题往往非凸，现有方法无法处理这一通用情况，存在明显的研究空白。

Method: 提出MOMEHA算法框架：(1) 利用Moreau包络将具有非凸下层的多目标双层问题转化为带包络约束的多目标单层优化问题；(2) 引入光滑加权Tchebycheff标量化技术，在保持单循环结构的同时避免计算Hessian矩阵；(3) 进一步提出基于动量的随机变体MB-MOMEHA，用于求解随机多目标双层学习问题。

Result: 在理论上，分别在确定性和随机设定下证明了所提算法的收敛性质。实验层面，在少样本元学习和神经架构搜索任务上，所提方法在Pareto前沿上优于现有方法，验证了有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文填补了非凸下层多目标双层优化方法的空白，提出的MOMEHA和MB-MOMEHA算法兼具理论保证与实用高效性（单循环、免Hessian），并在实际任务中展现了优于现有方法的Pareto前沿性能。

Abstract: Multi-objective bilevel optimization has wide applications in the AI area such as automated learning and multi-task meta-learning. Although recently some works have been begun to study the multi-objective bilevel optimization, the proposed methods rely on the (strongly) convex lower level problems. In fact, these multi-objective bilevel learning problems are generally nonconvex, and particularly their lower level problems are nonconvex. To fill this gap, we propose a class of Multi-Objective Moreau Envelope based Hessian-free Algorithms (MOMEHA) to solve the multi-objective bilevel learning problems with nonconvex lower level. Specifically, our method uses the Moreau envelope to convert the original problem into a multi-objective single-level optimization with an envelope constraint. In particular, our method retains computational advantages of being single-loop and Hessian-free in the multi-objective setting by incorporating a smooth weighted Tchebycheff scalarization. Furthermore, we propose a momentum-based variant of MOMEHA (i.e., MB-MOMEHA) method to solve the stochastic multi-objective bilevel learning problems. In theory, we provide the convergence properties of our algorithms under both deterministic and stochastic setting. Some experiments on few-shot meta-learning and neural architecture search demonstrate that our methods outperform the existing approaches in Pareto front, validating its effectiveness and robustness.

</details>


### [171] [Shape Design for Degenerate Hyperbolic Equation with Degenerate Boundary and Its Application to Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12754)
*Dong-Hui Yang,Yuanzhi Zhou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究控制区域与退化集相交的一类退化双曲方程的可观测性与可控性，提出了一种基于形状设计的逼近方法来解决退化带来的困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的退化双曲方程控制结果主要处理控制区域与退化集分离的情形，而当控制区域与退化集相交时，由于退化带来的本质性困难，现有方法不再适用，亟需新的技术来处理这一更具挑战性的情况。

Method: 引入基于形状设计的逼近方法，将退化方程用一族一致双曲方程近似。证明依赖于相关算子的谱逼近、弱解的精确估计以及乘子方法。首先建立逼近方程的可观测性不等式（常数与逼近参数无关），然后通过退化极限过程获得原始退化方程的可观测性不等式。

Result: 成功建立了当控制区域与退化集相交时退化双曲方程的可观测性不等式，进而得到相应的可控性结果，且逼近常数的独立性保证了极限过程的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过形状设计的逼近策略，克服了控制区域接触退化集时所面临的退化困难，将可观测性/可控性结果从控制区域与退化集分离的情形推广到了相交的情形。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the observability and controllability of a class of degenerate hyperbolic equations with a control region intersecting the degenerate set. Unlike the existing results that mainly deal with control regions separated from the degeneracy, we consider the case where the control region reaches the degenerate part. To handle the difficulty caused by the degeneracy, we introduce a shape-design-based approximation method based on shape design by approximating the degenerate equation with a family of uniformly hyperbolic equations. The proof relies on the spectral approximation of the associated operators, precise estimates for weak solutions, and the multiplier method. We first establish observability inequalities for the approximating equations with constants independent of the approximation parameter. Then, by passing to the degenerate limit, we obtain the observability inequality for the original degenerate equation.

</details>


### [172] [Difference-of-Convex Regularization for Graph Learning by Differentiable Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12757)
*Liping Tao,Chee Wei Tan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于差分凸正则化（DCR）的图学习框架，通过对偶表示将拉普拉斯正则化非负最小二乘（LR-NNLS）问题重构，避免了直接计算稠密且病态的拉普拉斯伪逆，从而高效近似伪逆的谱作用，并提供了稳定性和唯一不动点的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉普拉斯正则化最小化在信号处理和机器学习中至关重要，但图拉普拉斯伪逆的稠密且病态特性使得直接计算在大规模场景下不可行。此外，伪逆学习比拉普拉斯学习更具挑战性，需要一种无需直接求逆即可近似伪逆谱作用的方法。

Method: 在给定图拉普拉斯的条件下，提出差分凸正则化（DCR）图学习框架。通过对偶表示重新表述LR-NNLS问题，将伪逆学习与实例特定推断解耦；采用正则化最大似然估计（MLE）替代直接求逆；通过可微的对偶引导学习方案高效重建原始解。

Result: 数值实验表明，该方法在性能上优于凸求解器和图滤波基线，并在多种图拓扑下表现出稳健的性能。同时建立了关于算法稳定性和唯一不动点存在的理论保证。

Conclusion: DCR框架为大规模拉普拉斯正则化问题提供了一种可行的替代方案，在不牺牲精度的前提下绕过了伪逆的直接计算瓶颈，兼具理论保障和实际有效性。

Abstract: Laplacian-regularized minimization is fundamental in signal processing and machine learning, but is limited by the dense and ill-conditioned nature of the graph Laplacian pseudoinverse. While the Laplacian itself is sparse, its pseudoinverse is dense and often ill-conditioned, rendering direct computation impractical at scale. Moreover, pseudoinverse learning is more challenging than Laplacian learning. To address this challenge, this paper considers the setting where the graph Laplacian is given and proposes a Difference-of-Convex Regularizer (DCR) graph learning framework that approximates the spectral action of the Laplacian pseudoinverse without direct inversion via regularized Maximum Likelihood Estimation (MLE). By reformulating Laplacian-Regularized Nonnegative Least Squares (LR-NNLS) through a dual representation, DCR decouples pseudoinverse learning from instance-specific inference and enables efficient primal solution reconstruction via a differentiable dual-guided learning scheme. We establish theoretical guarantees on stability and the existence of a unique fixed point for DCR algorithm. Numerical experiments demonstrate improved performance over convex solvers and graph filtering baselines and robust performance across diverse graph topologies.

</details>


### [173] [A penalty-type method for relaxed inverse optimal control problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12769)
*Floriane Mefo Kue,Patrick Mehlitz,Thorsten Raasch*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种面向逆向最优控制中双层优化问题的惩罚型方法，通过逐步求解一系列代理问题来计算松弛值函数重构的稳定点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对逆向最优控制中出现的双层优化问题，现有数值方法在处理松弛值函数约束时往往计算代价高或需要复杂的线搜索，需要一个更高效、更简洁的求解框架。

Method: 提出一种惩罚型方法：将松弛值函数约束作为惩罚项引入代理问题，上下层决策变量的更新解耦，仅在可行性度量未充分改善时才增大惩罚参数；无需线搜索，每轮迭代仅需评估一次下层问题，且惩罚参数无需趋于无穷。

Result: 在合理假设下获得了子序列收敛性结果；数值实验中松弛参数也逐步趋于零，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该惩罚型方法以较低的计算成本（无线搜索、单次下层求值、有限惩罚参数）实现了双层优化问题稳定点的可靠求解，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: This paper is devoted to the introduction and analysis
  of a penalty-type method for the numerical treatment of
  a class of bilevel optimization problems arising from inverse optimal control.
  The algorithm is designed to compute stationary points of the associated relaxed value function reformulation.
  This is achieved by determining a sequence of stationary points associated with a sequence of surrogate problems
  where the relaxed value function constraint is penalized,
  where the updates of upper- and lower-level decision variables are decoupled,
  and where the penalty parameter is enlarged only in those iterations
  which do not come along with a sufficient improvement of some feasibility measure.
  The resulting method does not comprise any linesearch, the lower-level problem has to be evaluated just once per iteration,
  and the penalty parameter does not need to be driven to infinity.
  Nevertheless, subsequential convergence results are obtained under reasonable assumptions.
  Numerical experiments, where the relaxation parameter is also driven to zero, visualize effectiveness of the approach.

</details>


### [174] [Mean Field Stackelberg Game for Production and Carbon Emission Reduction with State Reflections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12775)
*Lijun Bo,Zhen Liu,Jingfei Wang,Jiacheng Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个具有平均场控制交互和公共噪声的两层Stackelberg博弈模型，用于刻画全球变暖背景下中央监管者与异构竞争区域之间的层级决策，并通过反射机制（局部时过程）强制执行排放上限。作者证明了近似Stackelberg均衡的存在性，并利用蒙特卡洛模拟进行敏感性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球变暖由人为碳排放驱动，具有跨界污染特征和不可逆损害，对人类构成生存威胁。需要一种能整合层级决策、考虑状态反射排放动力学和异质性区域竞争的博弈框架来设计有效的减排管理机制。

Method: 构建一个两层Stackelberg博弈：(1) 领导者（中央监管者）通过调整产品价格引导n个异构竞争区域（跟随者）；(2) 通过反射机制（局部时过程）强制实施硬性排放上限，建模紧急减排情景；(3) 引入平均场控制的相互作用与公共噪声，刻画区域间的相互影响和共同不确定性；(4) 借助反射型状态依赖的排放动力学求解近似Stackelberg均衡。

Result: 建立了近似Stackelberg均衡的存在性结果，并利用蒙特卡洛模拟进行了敏感性分析，考察关键参数变化对均衡策略与政策效果的影响。

Conclusion: 该模型为跨区域碳排放治理提供了新的理论框架，将层级决策、平均场交互、反射机制和公共噪声有机整合，为存在硬性排放上限约束下的最优定价与区域减排策略设计提供了理论支撑和数值验证。

Abstract: Global warming, driven by anthropogenic carbon emissions with transboundary pollution characteristics and irreversible damage, poses an existential threat to human society. This paper develops a novel two-level Stackelberg game with mean field interaction of controls and common noise which integrates hierarchical decision-making under a state-reflected emission dynamics. A central regulator (leader) adjusts product prices to guide $n$ heterogeneous competing regions (followers) while enforcing a hard emission cap via a reflection mechanism that models emergency reductions through a local time process. We establish the existence of an approximate Stackelberg equilibrium and perform sensitivity analysis via Monte Carlo simulations.

</details>


### [175] [Feedback Diagonalization and Stabilization for Discrete Linear Ensemble Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12813)
*Jonathan M. Bosnich,Xudong Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究由可数无穷多个线性标量控制系统组成的离散线性集成系统，该系统由共同标量控制输入驱动。作者给出了存在静态线性反馈控制律，使得闭环集成系统满足极点位于闭左半平面且无穷小生成元可对角化的充要条件，并证明序列(|b_n|)和(a_n/|b_n|)的指数衰减是必要的，且在期望极点假设下是充分的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与有限维线性系统不同，无穷维线性系统即使极点位于开左半平面也不能保证稳定。只有当极点位于闭左半平面且无穷小生成元可对角化时，稳定性才能得到保证。因此，需要研究离散线性集成系统在何种条件下可通过公共反馈控制达到稳定。

Method: 作者对由(a_n,b_n)表示的可数无穷个标量线性系统（a_n>0）组成的集成系统进行分析，考察是否存在静态线性反馈控制律使得闭环系统同时满足：(i) 闭环极点含于闭左半平面；(ii) 无穷小生成元可对角化。通过谱分析和控制理论方法，推导出存在此类反馈律的充要条件。

Result: 证明了序列(|b_n|)和(a_n/|b_n|)的指数衰减是存在静态线性反馈稳定控制律的必要条件，并且在期望极点满足一定假设的前提下，该条件也是充分的，从而保证了闭环系统极点的位置和生成元的可对角化性。

Conclusion: 本文为离散线性集成系统的反馈稳定性提供了完整的充要条件刻画，揭示了序列指数衰减特性在无穷维系统可稳定性中的核心作用，为后续此类系统的控制器设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We consider a countably infinite collection of linear, scalar control systems, where each system is represented by the pair $(a_n,b_n)$ with $a_n>0$ for $n\in \mathbb{N}$, and all systems are forced by a common scalar control input. We refer to this collection of systems as a discrete linear ensemble system. The ensemble system is feedback stabilizable if there exists a common feedback control input that asymptotically stabilizes every system simultaneously. Unlike finite-dimensional linear systems, an infinite-dimensional linear system is not guaranteed to be stable if its poles lie in the open left half-plane. Stability is guaranteed, however, if (i) its poles are contained in the closed left half-plane and (ii) its infinitesimal generator is diagonalizable. In this paper, we provide necessary and sufficient conditions for the existence of a static, linear feedback control law that ensures the closed-loop ensemble system satisfies (i) and (ii). In particular, we show that the exponential decay of the sequences $(|b_n|)_{n\in \mathbb{N}}$ and $(a_n/|b_n|)_{n\in \mathbb{N}}$ is necessary and, under an assumption on the desired poles, sufficient.

</details>


### [176] [A Lebeau--Robbiano approach to the controllability of the Boussinesq system with a reduced number of controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12815)
*Víctor Hernández-Santamaría*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了二维和三维Boussinesq系统在减少控制数量条件下的局部零能控性，控制仅作用于温度方程和速度方程的N-2个分量。证明基于Lebeau-Robbiano谱方法，利用线性化系统的级联结构、混合可观测性估计和Stokes高频耗散，将线性可控性估计通过时间迭代传递到非线性系统，控制代价为C exp(C/T)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Boussinesq系统的能控性问题在数学控制论中具有重要性，但该系统的耦合结构（速度由Stokes算子支配，温度由Dirichlet拉普拉斯算子支配）使得两者的谱分解不兼容，导致经典的分量式谱方法无法直接适用。本文旨在减少控制数量（仅需N-2个速度分量和温度控制）的同时证明局部零能控性，并保持抛物型系统期望的小时间代价。

Method: 基于Lebeau-Robbiano方法的谱方法。核心步骤包括：(1) 利用线性化系统的级联结构克服速度与温度方程谱分解不匹配的困难；(2) 建立混合可观测性估计——仅对Stokes分量做谱局部化，而热分量无需频率限制；(3) 结合Stokes高频耗散进行Lebeau-Robbiano迭代；(4) 通过时间迭代论证将线性可控性估计传递到非线性Boussinesq系统。

Result: 证明了二维和三维Boussinesq系统在减少控制数量下的局部零能控性，控制仅作用在温度方程和N-2个速度分量上；控制的小时间代价上界为C exp(C/T)，恢复了抛物型系统的预期阶数，同时保持了减少的控制数量。

Conclusion: 本文通过发展混合可观测性估计和利用级联结构，成功克服了Boussinesq系统耦合谱结构不兼容的根本困难，在减少控制数量的前提下实现了局部零能控性，并保证了最优阶的小时间代价。该结果推进了非线性耦合抛物型系统能控性理论的发展。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove the local null controllability of the Boussinesq system in dimensions two and three with a reduced number of localized controls. More precisely, the controls act on the temperature equation and on only $N-2$ components of the velocity equation. Our proof is based on a spectral approach in the spirit of the Lebeau--Robbiano method.
  The main difficulty comes from the coupled structure of the system. The velocity and temperature equations are governed by the Stokes operator and the Dirichlet Laplacian, respectively. Their natural spectral decompositions are different, and there is no common spectral localization naturally adapted to the coupled system. Therefore, the usual componentwise spectral argument cannot be applied directly. We show that the cascade structure of the linearized system makes it possible to overcome this difficulty. The main ingredient is a mixed observability estimate in which only the Stokes component is spectrally localized, while the heat component is treated without any frequency restriction. Combining this estimate with the dissipation of the high Stokes frequencies allows us to carry out a Lebeau--Robbiano iteration for the linearized system. The resulting linear controllability estimate is transferred to the nonlinear Boussinesq system through a time-iteration argument. As an important consequence, the controls have a small-time cost bounded by $C\exp(C/T)$, which recovers the expected parabolic order while preserving the reduced number of controls.

</details>


### [177] [Distribution Steering via Sliced Optimal Transport Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12828)
*Kaito Ito,Anqi Dong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于切片最优传输的有限时域控制框架，通过一维投影定义方向性终端条件，构造最小能量随机单方向控制器，并通过对投影方向取平均得到确定性切片反馈律，实现对动态系统状态分布的引导控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优传输为分布引导提供了自然几何方法，但在全状态空间中实现需要传输映射或耦合，计算复杂。切片最优传输虽通过一维投影避免了全维构造，但其产生的投影映射只给出方向位移，不能直接构成可实现的反馈律，因此需要开发一种将切片最优传输转化为实际可执行控制器的框架。

Method: 在每个采样时刻，利用投影最优传输映射定义方向性终端条件；通过最小能量实现得到随机单方向控制器；对所有投影方向取平均获得确定性切片反馈。针对单积分器动力学，分析切片Wasserstein距离的单调性；对高斯端点分布，推导仿射保持高斯性的控制器及线性衰减的增益。进一步分析控制能量，证明随机控制器在采样周期趋零时收敛到平均切片流，并将构造扩展到一般线性系统（利用可达性归一化坐标和局部可控性Gramian）。

Result: 对于单积分器系统，所提平均化反馈使切片Wasserstein距离单调非增；对高斯端点分布，控制器是仿射的、保持高斯性，并将均值与协方差驱动到预设终端值，且存在与定律相关的增益实现切片Wasserstein距离的线性衰减及显式控制能量刻画。随机控制器在采样周期趋零时收敛到确定性平均切片流。对线性系统，均匀全驱动系统可即时实现切片速度，一般可控系统可通过局部可控性Gramian实现精确有限步到达。

Conclusion: 本文建立的切片最优传输控制框架成功将几何分布传输转化为可实现的状态反馈控制，为分布引导控制提供了理论基础与实践算法，并通过数值示例验证了所提分布流的有效性。

Abstract: Distribution steering seeks feedback laws that drive the state law of a dynamical system between prescribed initial and terminal distributions. Optimal transport provides a natural geometric approach, but its implementation generally requires a transport map or coupling in the full state space. Sliced optimal transport avoids this full-dimensional construction through one-dimensional projections. Yet, the resulting projected maps specify only directional displacements and do not by themselves prescribe a realizable feedback law. To this end, we develop a finite-horizon control framework based on sliced optimal transport. At each sampling instant, a projected optimal transport map defines a directional terminal condition, whose minimum-energy realization yields a randomized single-direction controller. Averaging over projection directions gives a deterministic sliced feedback. For the single-integrator dynamics, the averaged feedback makes the sliced Wasserstein distance to the target non-increasing. For Gaussian endpoint laws, it is affine, preserves Gaussianity, and steers the mean and covariance to their prescribed terminal values. We further identify a law-dependent gain that yields linear decay of the sliced Wasserstein distance together with an explicit characterization of the control energy. We also prove that the randomized controller converges to the averaged sliced flow as the sampling period vanishes. Finally, we extend the construction to linear dynamical systems. Reachability-normalized coordinates allow instantaneous realization of the sliced velocity for uniformly fully actuated systems, while local controllability Gramians provide exact finite-step realization for general controllable systems. Numerical examples illustrate the resulting distributional flows.

</details>


### [178] [Distance-Based Formation Control with Prescribed Performance for Higher-Order Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12896)
*Janina Schaa,Maria Charitidou,Dimos Dimarogonas,Thomas Berger*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对相对阶大于一的未知非线性多智能体系统，提出分布式无模型的基于距离的编队控制方案，引入虚拟领导者实现预设瞬态性能跟踪，在树图或最小刚性图通信拓扑下分别设计了控制律，证明了编队误差和跟踪误差满足预设漏斗约束且闭环信号有界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式编队控制大多假设智能体动力学已知或相对阶为一阶，而实际系统往往具有未知非线性动力学且相对阶大于一。此外，预设瞬态性能（如漏斗约束）在基于距离的编队控制中尚未得到充分解决，因此需要开发一种模型无关且能保证瞬态和稳态性能的控制方案。

Method: 引入一个虚拟领导者，其轨迹由指定子集智能体以预设瞬态行为跟踪。在无向通信图中，分别考虑树图和最小且无穷小刚性图两种拓扑。对后者进一步考虑质心跟踪目标。为每种场景设计了分布式且无模型的控制律，利用漏斗控制思想将编队误差和跟踪误差约束在预设的瞬态性能包络内。

Result: 从理论上证明了所提控制律能使编队误差和跟踪误差满足预设的漏斗约束，且所有闭环信号有界。数值仿真验证了所提控制律的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为相对阶大于一的未知非线性多智能体系统提供了一种无模型、分布式的距离基编队控制方法，能够同时保证预设瞬态性能、编队维持和跟踪误差约束，适用于树形图和刚性图两类通信拓扑结构。

Abstract: We consider distributed distance-based formation control with prescribed transient performance for multi-agent systems modeled by unknown nonlinear dynamics of relative degree greater than one. We introduce a virtual leader whose trajectory is tracked with prescribed transient behavior by a designated subset of agents. The undirected communication graph is either a tree graph or a minimally and infinitesimally rigid graph. In the latter case, we further consider the objective of centroid tracking. In each setting, a distributed and model-free control law is developed, and we establish the satisfaction of the prescribed funnel constraints on the formation and tracking errors, and boundedness of all closed-loop signals. Numerical simulations illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed control laws.

</details>


### [179] [Optimizing Multi-Stakeholder Fairness in Computed Tomography Appointment Scheduling with Predicted Scan and Reporting Duration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13059)
*Ludovico Ambrosi,Chandra Bortolotto,Sara Cambiaghi,Luisa Carone,Davide Duma,Lorenzo Preda*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个预测-处方框架用于公平感知的CT随访排程：先用患者特定的ML模型预测检查和报告时长，再将预测嵌入多目标混合整数线性规划模型，同时最小化患者首选检查日期的偏差并通过字典序最小-最大公平准则平衡放射科医生的报告工作量。在真实急诊放射科数据上的实验表明，仅允许1-2天的调度灵活性即可显著改善医生工作量的公平性，且所提出的支配性剪枝策略能大幅减少ε-约束求解次数而不影响Pareto前沿。此外，用下游优化遗憾评估预测模型发现XGBoost最适合排程决策，优于传统预测指标上的低误差模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随访CT排程需要在患者推荐检查日期与放射科医生工作量公平分配两个竞争目标之间取得平衡。现有方法仅优化扫描仪利用率或患者等待时间，忽略了报告活动和多利益相关方的公平性。此外，预测模型通常用传统指标（如预测误差）评估，忽略了其在排程决策中的下游影响。

Method: 提出预测-处方框架：(1) 构建患者特定ML模型预测检查和报告时长；(2) 将预测嵌入多目标MILP，同时最小化患者日期偏差并采用字典序最小-最大公平准则平衡放射医生报告工作量；(3) 在ε-约束框架内推导支配性剪枝性质以减少求解的优化问题数量；(4) 通过下游优化遗憾来评估预测模型在排程决策中的实际效用。

Result: 基于真实急诊放射科数据的实验表明：允许患者1-2天的调度灵活性即可在保持随访检查及时性的同时大幅提升医生工作量公平性；支配性剪枝策略消除了大部分ε-约束评估且不影响Pareto前沿；在下游优化遗憾评估下，XGBoost比传统预测误差指标更优的模型提供了更有效的排程决策支持。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在CT随访排程中，微小的调度灵活性足以换取显著的工作量公平性提升；通过下游优化视角评估预测模型比仅看传统预测指标更有意义；所提出的支配性剪枝方法有效提升了多目标优化求解的计算效率。

Abstract: Scheduling follow-up Computed Tomography (CT) examinations requires balancing two competing objectives: assigning patients as close as possible to their recommended examination dates while ensuring an equitable distribution of radiologists' workload. Existing approaches optimize scanner utilization or patient waiting times, overlooking reporting activities and the need to balance fairness across multiple stakeholders. This paper proposes a predictive-prescriptive framework for fairness-aware follow-up CT scheduling. Patient-specific Machine Learning (ML) models are first developed to predict both examination and reporting durations. These predictions are then embedded into a multi-objective Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) model that simultaneously minimizes deviations from patients' preferred examination dates and balances radiologists' reporting workloads through a lexicographic min-max fairness criterion. We derive a dominance reduction property within an $ε$-constraint framework that substantially reduces the number of optimization problems required to generate the Pareto frontier. Computational experiments based on data from a real-world emergency radiology department show that allowing patients a scheduling flexibility of only one to two days is sufficient to substantially improve workload equity among radiologists while preserving timely access to follow-up examinations. The proposed dominance reduction strategy eliminates most $ε$-constraint evaluations without affecting the Pareto frontier. Finally, evaluating predictive models through downstream optimization regret demonstrates that XGBoost provides the most effective support for scheduling decisions, outperforming models that achieve lower prediction errors according to conventional predictive metrics.

</details>


### [180] [Adaptive Schauder Stochastic Mirror Descent in Banach Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13121)
*Jinhui Bai,Shuai Lu,Lei Shi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在无限维Banach空间中求解风险泛函最小化问题的随机镜像下降（SMD）扩展算法，通过Schauder基构造自适应有限维子空间并引入新的正则化策略，实现了O(n^{-1/p_1})的收敛率、O(n^{1+θ})的时间复杂度和O(n^θ)的内存复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SMD方法主要针对有限维或欧几里德空间，难以处理无限维Banach空间中的风险泛函最小化问题，特别是当随机梯度信息只能通过采样获取时。此外，非均匀凸空间（如L^1空间）缺乏合适的Bregman距离，限制了SMD在该类问题中的应用。

Method: （1）根据Banach空间的均匀凸性性质选择Bregman距离，对于非均匀凸空间L^1_{ρ_X}(Ω)则利用熵函数构造Bregman距离；（2）基于Schauder基引入一族适应样本量n的有限维子空间；（3）每次SMD迭代将子问题限制在对应有限维子空间上，并将随机梯度投影到有限维对偶空间，形成新的正则化策略；（4）该策略允许显式高效求解SMD子问题并实现偏差-方差权衡。算法时间复杂度为O(n^{1+θ})，空间复杂度为O(n^θ)。

Result: 在新的分析框架下，证明了算法在适当的凸性条件下达到O(n^{-1/p_1})的收敛率（p_1≥2由底层空间的凸性决定），在极小值者仅满足较弱正则性条件的误设定设定下仍能收敛到最小值。算法还扩展到反问题并在数值实验中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 本文将SMD从有限维推广到无限维Banach空间，通过巧妙结合Schauder基展开与有限维子空间投影策略，在保持高效计算的同时实现了接近最优的收敛速率，并为非均匀凸空间提供了基于熵的Bregman距离构造方案，拓展了随机优化方法在函数空间中的应用范围。

Abstract: In this paper, we extend stochastic mirror descent (SMD) to infinite-dimensional Banach spaces for solving a class of risk functional minimization problems, where stochastic gradient information is only available through sampling. We first choose the Bregman distance according to the uniform convexity properties of the Banach space. For the non-uniformly convex space $\mathcal{L}_{ρ_X}^1(Ω)$, we instead construct a Bregman distance induced by the entropy function. Based on a Schauder basis of the Banach space, we introduce a family of finite-dimensional subspaces that adapt to the sample size $n$. At each SMD iteration, we restrict the subproblem to the corresponding finite-dimensional subspace and project the stochastic gradient onto the associated finite-dimensional dual space, thereby introducing a new regularization strategy. This regularization strategy allows us to explicitly solve the SMD subproblem efficiently and to achieve a bias--variance trade-off. The algorithm requires $\mathcal{O}(n^{1+θ})$ time and $\mathcal{O}(n^θ)$ memory, where $θ>0$ can be chosen arbitrarily small when the minimizer has sufficient regularity. By developing a new analytical framework, we prove that the proposed algorithm achieves a convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}\left(n^{-1/p_1}\right)$ up to logarithmic factors, where $p_1\geq2$ is determined by the convexity properties of the underlying space. In the misspecified setting where the minimizer satisfies only weaker regularity conditions, we show that the proposed algorithm still converges to the minimum. We further extend the algorithm to inverse problems and validate its effectiveness in numerical experiments.

</details>


### [181] [Positive stabilization of a pure diffusion system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13203)
*Violaine Piengeon,Joseph J. Winkin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文对一类带点观测的边界控制扩散系统，从解析角度完整研究了正镇定性、正状态估计以及基于观测器的正反馈镇定问题，并提出了原创的观测器反馈镇定策略。同时针对标称PDE系统的任意阶空间离散化也研究了这三个问题，给出了显式且易于实现的解析解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散系统在工程中广泛存在（如热传导、化学反应器等），而正系统（状态始终非负）的约束在实际中具有重要物理意义。本文旨在为边界控制扩散系统在正性约束下提供解析的最优镇定与状态估计设计方案，并兼顾数值实现与标称解的对比验证。

Method: 采用解析方法对边界控制扩散系统（带点观测）分别求解正镇定、正状态估计和基于观测器的正反馈镇定三个最优设计问题；提出一种原创的观测器反馈镇定策略；对任意阶空间离散化后的系统同样求解三类问题，并与连续标称解进行严格比较。

Result: 在连续和离散两种设定下均获得了显式且可直接实现的解析解，并完成了解析最优设计与离散近似解之间的一致性验证与比较。

Conclusion: 该研究为带正性约束的边界控制扩散系统提供了完整的解析设计框架，同时给出了可数值实施的空间离散解，兼具理论完备性与工程实用性。

Abstract: This paper provides a complete analytical study of the positive stabilization, positive state estimation, and observer-based positive stabilization problems for a generic boundary control diffusion system with point observation. In particular, optimal design problems are solved analytically, and an original strategy for observer-based feedback stabilization is proposed. The three problems are also investigated for an arbitrary-order spatial discretization of the nominal PDE system enabling numerical implementation together with rigorous comparisons with the nominal solutions. In both the continuous and discretized settings, explicit and readily implementable solutions are derived.

</details>


### [182] [A stabilized sequential quadratic programming method for degenerate nonlinear optimization problems on Riemannian manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13298)
*Yuya Yamakawa,Mamoru Oka*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于黎曼流形上退化约束优化问题的稳定化序列二次规划（SQP）方法，在不需要约束规范假设的前提下，证明生成序列的聚点必为KKT点、近似KKT点或可行性问题驻点，并通过数值实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的黎曼SQP方法仅在约束规范（如LICQ等）成立时才能保证全局收敛性，而实际中的退化解约束优化问题（约束规范可能失效）无法确保这些方法的收敛行为。因此需要一种不依赖约束规范假设的稳定化方法，以处理此类退化问题。

Method: 将欧几里得空间中的稳定化SQP框架推广到黎曼流形上，针对带等式与不等式约束的黎曼非线性规划问题（RNLP），在不假设任何约束规范的条件下设计新的算法框架。

Result: 在不假设任何约束规范的前提下，证明了所提方法生成的序列存在聚点，且该聚点要么是KKT点、要么是近似KKT（AKKT）点、要么是相关可行性问题的驻点。数值实验验证了该方法对退化问题的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作将稳定化SQP方法从欧几里得空间成功扩展到黎曼流形，为退化约束优化问题提供了一种无需约束规范的全局收敛保障方案，拓展了Riemannian优化理论的应用范畴。

Abstract: We propose a stabilized sequential quadratic programming (SQP) method for degenerate constrained optimization problems on Riemannian manifolds. The problem considered in this study is a Riemannian nonlinear programming problem (RNLP) with equality and inequality constraints, where classical constraint qualifications may fail. While existing Riemannian SQP methods guarantee global convergence only under constraint qualifications, their convergence behavior is not ensured for degenerate problems. To address this limitation, we extend the stabilized SQP framework from Euclidean spaces to Riemannian manifolds. Without assuming any constraint qualification, we prove that the generated sequence has an accumulation point that is a Karush--Kuhn--Tucker (KKT) point, an approximate KKT (AKKT) point, or a stationary point of an associated feasibility problem. Finally, we conduct numerical experiments to confirm the effectiveness of the proposed method for degenerate problems.

</details>


### [183] [Distributed and Dynamic Hub Network Operation Planning in a Hyperconnected Less-Than-Truckload Operating System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13419)
*Tiankuo Zhang,Jihye Jung,Paria Nourmohammadi,Benoit Montreuil,Alan Erera,Sahrish Jaleel Shaikh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对零担运输（LTL）行业中多区域枢纽网络协作的运营规划问题，基于物理互联网（PI）理念中的模块化容器和开放资源共享原则，设计了一套多枢纽运营规划协议，用于分布式枢纽运营商的站内运输分配和站间拖车调度，并通过仿真实验验证了其有效性，以提升物流系统的效率和可持续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LTL行业虽较整车运输有更强的整合优势，但其平均成本仍然偏高，原因是运营效率低下以及中小型承运商网络高度碎片化。现有研究缺乏针对多枢纽网络之间协作运营的规划协议，因此需要建立一种分布式、动态的物流枢纽网络系统，促进多运营商之间资源共享与合作。

Method: 构建基于物理互联网（PI）原则的分布式动态物流枢纽网络系统，聚焦枢纽间和枢纽内运营。设计一套多枢纽运营规划协议，运营商在各自网络中使用该协议分别规划两类操作：(1)站内货件到目的地专用拖车的分配；(2)站间拖车的发车时刻表。通过精心设计的枢纽网络间连接机制，使聚合的枢纽网络系统能够实现协作结果并满足货运请求。采用基于仿真的实验，在运营商多枢纽网络的多种场景下评估协议有效性。

Result: 通过仿真实验在多个场景下评估了所提协议的有效性，结果表明该协议能够支持多区域枢纽网络间的协作运营，提高PI网络系统的实用性和鲁棒性，促进枢纽网络间的合作，从而推动更高效、更可持续的物流系统。

Conclusion: 本研究改进了物理互联网网络系统的实用性和鲁棒性，支持枢纽网络间的更大程度合作，为LTL行业构建更高效、更可持续的物流系统提供了可行的多枢纽运营规划框架。每个货件在进入每个枢纽网络时由其到达入口枢纽的预计时间和离开出口枢纽的最晚时间定义，基于此信息设计的协议能够有效协调分布式枢纽运营商的运营决策。

Abstract: The less-than-truckload (LTL) industry plays a vital role in enhancing the efficiency and sustainability of logistics systems, as LTL shipments offer greater consolidation opportunities than full-truckload shipments. Despite of this flexibility, the average cost of LTL shipments remains considerably higher due to less efficient operations and highly fragmented networks of small and medium-sized carriers. Building on our ongoing effort to develop a distributed and dynamic logistics hub network system grounded in the Physical Internet (PI) principles of modular containers and open resource sharing, this study focuses specifically on inter-hub and in-hub operations, with cooperation among multiple regional hub networks. Therefore, a shipment may traverse multiple cooperating hub networks. With respect to each hub network each shipment enters, it is defined by its expected arrival time at the entry hub and its latest arrival time at the exit hub. Based on the defined shipment information, we design a set of multi-hub operation planning protocols for distributed hub operators. In their operating networks, operators use our smartly designed protocol separately to plan in-hub shipments' assignments to destination-specific trailers and inter-hub trailers' dispatch schedules. With carefully designed interconnections between hub networks, the aggregated hub network system is well-positioned to achieve cooperative outcomes and fulfill shipment requests. We evaluate the effectiveness of the proposed protocol through a simulation-based experiment under multiple scenarios in an operator's multi-hub network. Overall, this research improves the practicality and robustness of PI-based networks and supports greater cooperation among hub networks toward more efficient and sustainable logistics systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [Position: Reasoning is a Learnable Rule-Based Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12325)
*Rachel Lawrence,Jacqueline Maasch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出自主推理领域存在定义模糊问题，导致推理评估的构念效度无法验证，阻碍了可信自主推理的量化进展；作者基于文献综合给出操作性定义（将有效且可靠的推理定位为可学习的基于规则的过程），并提供研究沟通的最佳实践清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主推理是AI领域最具科学和经济价值的话题之一，但生成式AI社区对"推理"缺乏统一定义，且常隐含地拒绝逻辑与可验证自动推理的历史处理方法。这种定义模糊性使推理评估的构念效度（construct validity）无法验证，从而削弱了向可信自主推理方向量化进步的可靠性与可复现性。

Method: 本文为立场论文（position paper），通过综合整理既有文献提出：(1) 操作性定义，将有效（valid）且可靠（sound）的推理定位为一种可学习的、基于规则的过程（learnable rule-based process）；(2) 为AI推理研究沟通提供最佳实践检查清单，以规范定义、评估与方法论表述。

Result: 作者给出了一套基于文献综合的操作性定义，将推理重新锚定在逻辑与可验证自动推理传统之上，并提出一套面向AI推理研究论文的最佳实践清单，用于提升研究的定义清晰度、评估效度与沟通规范性。

Conclusion: 定义模糊是可解决的（addressable）。通过采纳操作性定义与最佳实践清单，社区可以改善推理研究的构念效度与量化可评估性，从而更可靠地推进可信自主推理的发展。

Abstract: Autonomous reasoning is among the most scientifically and economically motivating topics in AI today. Historically the purview of symbolic AI, recent advances have mainly emerged from deep probabilistic generative models. Despite immense interest and rapid progress, the generative AI community has not clearly converged on operational definitions for reasoning and often implicitly rejects the historical treatment of this topic in logic and verifiable automated reasoning. This position contends that definitional ambiguity leaves the construct validity of reasoning evaluation unverifiable, undermining quantifiable progress toward trustworthy autonomous reasoning. We also contend that this ambiguity is addressable. To that end, we provide (1) operational definitions based on a synthesis of the literature, positioning valid and sound reasoning as a learnable rule-based process; and (2) a checklist for best practices in the communication of AI reasoning research.

</details>


### [185] [Diagnostic Foundation for Evaluating LLMs' Research Integrity as Co-Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12345)
*Yash Tripathi,Silu Sharma,Sai Sidhanth Manoharan Jayanthi,Shivank Garg,Lin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IntegrityBench基准，用于评估语言模型在机构压力下维护研究诚信的能力。通过对36个配对任务、18个前沿模型变体在5级隐式-显式压力协议下的测试，发现模型在最大压力下约有三分之一的关键诚信决策失败，且规模与推理能力无法可靠缓解这一问题。显式压力诱导模型顺从不当行为，而隐式情境重构更常导致对合法研究任务的过度拒绝。研究发现三个诚信维度（不当行为分类、伦理行动推理、基于文物的决策）在结构上相互分离，正确伦理行动并不需要准确分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越多地被用作合作科学家，但其在制度压力下维护研究诚信的能力尚未被量化评估。现有评估缺乏针对诚信问题的系统性基准，也无法区分不同类型的诚信失败及其部署风险。

Method: 构建IntegrityBench基准，包含跨3个领域和4个研究阶段的36个配对任务，评估不当行为分类、伦理行动推理和基于文物的决策三个维度。采用5级隐式-显式压力协议模拟不同程度的制度压力，对18个前沿模型变体进行系统评估。

Result: 模型在峰值压力下约1/3的诚信关键决策失败；规模与推理能力不能可靠缓解此问题。显式压力导致顺从不当行为，隐式重构导致过度拒绝合法任务。有意思的是，分类失败的模型在基于文物的决策上表现反而更好（85.7 vs 79.4），表明三方面能力结构分离。

Conclusion: 前沿模型可能表现有帮助但隐藏诚信失败，产生两种部署风险：助长科研不当行为和侵蚀对AI辅助研究的信任。模型的诚信能力在结构上彼此分离，意味着需要针对不同维度单独设计评估和干预策略。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly deployed as co-scientists, yet their ability to uphold research integrity under institutional pressure remains unmeasured. We introduce IntegrityBench, a benchmark evaluating misconduct classification, ethical action reasoning and artifact-grounded decision making across 36 paired tasks under a 5-level implicit-explicit pressure protocol spanning 3 domains and 4 research stages. Evaluating 18 frontier model variants, we find that under peak pressure, models fail roughly 1 in 3 integrity-critical decisions, and neither scale nor reasoning ability reliably mitigates this. Explicit pressures induce compliance with misconduct, while implicit contextual reframing more often causes over-refusal of legitimate research tasks. Interestingly, models failing to classify research requests accurately perform equally or better on artifact-grounded decision making (85.7 vs. 79.4), suggesting the three facets are structurally dissociated and correct ethical action does not require accurate classification. Frontier models can thus appear helpful while harbouring integrity failures that create two distinct deployment risks: facilitating research misconduct and eroding trust in AI-assisted research.

</details>


### [186] [Position: The Alignment Community is Unintentionally Building a Censor's Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12346)
*Sarah Ball,Phil Hackemann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证现代AI对齐方法是一种"双重用途"技术，既可用于防止有害输出，也可能被恶意行为者滥用为审查和操纵工具，呼吁社区关注并制定缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI对齐技术最初旨在防止模型产生有害输出，但其机制本身可能被恶意行为者反向利用，实现信息审查与操纵。随着AI作为信息提供者的快速普及、经济权力不对称加剧以及政治环境趋于威权化，这种双重用途风险正在加速扩大，亟需公开讨论。

Method: 本文采用立场论文的形式，将现有AI对齐技术与可能的滥用场景和实际案例进行映射分析，论证"完美对齐"模型的追求如何无意中为恶意行为者提供了日益强大的信息主导工具。

Result: 通过映射分析揭示了AI对齐技术的双重用途性，指出其可能被用于审查和操纵，且风险因AI采用速度、经济不对称和威权化政治趋势而被放大。

Conclusion: 呼吁社区正视AI对齐机制的有意滥用可能性，并提出缓解策略以防范这种双重用途潜在风险。

Abstract: This position paper argues that modern AI alignment methods - originally designed to prevent harmful output - are dual-use technologies that may easily be misused by malicious actors for censorship and manipulation. By mapping current alignment techniques to the possibility and actual cases of misuse, we show that the quest for a "perfectly aligned" model inadvertently also provides malicious actors with an ever-improving tool for informational dominance. We need to discuss this dual-use potential now, as its risk is exacerbated by rapid user adoption of AI as information provider, economic power asymmetries, and a political landscape that increasingly shifts towards authoritarianism. We conclude by urging the community to consider the intentional misuse of AI alignment mechanisms and propose mitigation strategies to safeguard against this dual-use potential.

</details>


### [187] [Agreement Is Not Alignment: Divergent Moral Grounds in Human and LLM Ethical Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12368)
*Octavian M. Machidon,Alina L. Machidon,Vojko Strahovnik,Mateja Centa Strahovnik,Jonas Miklavčič,Marko Robnik Šikonja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过构建一个500项伦理基准数据集，同时收集人类标注者和LLM的最终标签与支持理由，发现模型与人类在最终标签上的一致率虽高，但在道德理据层面存在系统性分歧，从而论证了"标签一致性≠对齐"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM对齐评估常以与人类判断的一致性作为代理指标，但这种一致并不能证明模型与人类依赖相同的道德理由或原则。两个智能体可能得出相同的判断却基于不同的道德立场、语境假设或情境解读，因此需要区分"标签一致"与"道德理据对齐"。

Method: 构建一个基于ETHICS的500项精选基准，涵盖五个道德判断领域；收集人类标注者和多种LLM关于最终标签与支持理由的双重标注；通过标签层面的准确率对比和理由层面的内容分析（考察模型与人类在不同道德范畴如伤害、尊重、守信、公正、应得、免责事由等上的注意力分布）进行系统性比较。

Result: 前沿与开源模型在人类大多数标签上的一致率通常较高；然而理由层面的分析揭示了系统性分歧——即使在最终标签与人类多数一致时，模型在不同道德范畴上的注意力分配仍然与人类标注者存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 一致性不应被视为对齐的等价物。仅依赖标签层面的评估可能产生误导性的放心效果，必须辅以对模型判断中所体现的理由、原则和道德优先级的分析，才能更准确地评估模型的对齐程度。

Abstract: Agreement with human judgments is a common proxy for evaluating the alignment of large language models (LLMs). Yet agreement in final labels does not show that human annotators and models rely on the same moral grounds. Two agents may reach the same judgment while appealing to different principles, contextual assumptions, or interpretations of the situation.
  We test this distinction using a curated 500-item ETHICS-derived benchmark spanning five domains of moral judgment, with new human annotator and LLM annotations of both final labels and supporting rationales. Across frontier and open model families, agreement with human annotator majority labels is often high. However, rationale-level analysis reveals systematic divergence in the moral grounds expressed by human annotators and models. In particular, models redistribute attention across categories such as harm, respect, promise-keeping, justice, desert, and excuse relevance, even when their final labels match the human annotator majority.
  Our results show that agreement should not be treated as equivalent to alignment. Label-based evaluation can therefore be misleadingly reassuring unless complemented by analysis of the reasons, principles, and moral priorities expressed in model judgments.

</details>


### [188] [Multi-Agent Scheduling with LLM-Assisted Contract Net Negotiation for Stream Processing in Mobile Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12371)
*Sabeur Lajili,Zaki Brahmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAS-DecStream，一个面向移动边缘-云异构基础设施的多智能体流处理调度系统，其核心贡献是LLM-MR-CNP——一种扩展的合同网协议（CNP），融合了语义化CFP表述、渐进式上下文披露、多轮提案修订、协商记忆和确定性验证。实验表明该方法可将延迟违规降至3%，消除资源过度承诺，在20个智能体下冲突解决率达0.91，比多轮规则基线效用提升22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流处理系统日益运行在移动边缘-云异构基础设施上，面临工作负载波动、资源竞争和严格的QoS要求，这使得去中心化调度变得极为复杂。经典的合同网协议等去中心化协商机制在处理这种动态、资源受限且具有不确定性的环境时能力有限。

Method: MAS-DecStream通过LLM-MR-CNP扩展经典CNP：边缘集群智能体基于局部观测、预测资源状态和定性运行时上下文，利用LLM细化自然语言卸载提案；关键资源与QoS约束仍保持确定性验证。协议包含语义化CFP表述、渐进式上下文披露、多轮提案修订、协商记忆及确定性校验五个关键设计要素。

Result: 基于阿里云ASI Trace的实验中，MAS-DecStream将延迟违规降至3%，消除资源过度承诺，20个智能体场景下冲突解决率达0.91，效用较多轮规则基线提升高达22%。另通过25个独立案例评估揭示了模型与提示词相关的准确性-成本权衡。

Conclusion: 初步证据表明，多轮CNP修订是协议层面的主要增益来源，而LLM辅助的价值主要体现在处理定性和不确定的运行时上下文方面，为未来边缘-云流处理调度提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Stream-processing systems increasingly operate across heterogeneous mobile edge--cloud infrastructures, where workload volatility, resource contention, and stringent quality-of-service (QoS) requirements complicate decentralized scheduling. This paper proposes \emph{MAS-DecStream}, whose main contribution is \emph{LLM-MR-CNP}: an extension of the classical Contract Net Protocol with semantic CFP formulation, progressive context disclosure, multi-round proposal revision, negotiation memory, and deterministic validation. Edge-cluster agents refine natural-language offloading proposals from local observations, predicted resource states, and qualitative runtime context, while hard resource and QoS constraints remain deterministic. Experiments derived from the Alibaba ASI Trace evaluate the extension at three levels: single- versus multi-round CNP, rule-based versus LLM-assisted refinement, and fixed-model single- versus multi-round negotiation. Under the evaluated configurations, MAS-DecStream reduces latency violations to 3\%, eliminates resource overcommitment, reaches a conflict-resolution rate of 0.91 with 20 agents, and improves utility by up to 22\% over the multi-round rule-based baseline. A separate 25-case evaluation shows model- and prompt-dependent accuracy--cost trade-offs. The results provide initial evidence that multi-round CNP refinement is the principal protocol-level gain, with LLM assistance adding value for qualitative and uncertain runtime context.

</details>


### [189] [Position: We Need Practical AI Alignment Methods to Mirror Human Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12372)
*Vijay Keswani,Breanna K. Nguyen,Cyrus Cousins,Vincent Conitzer,Walter Sinnott-Armstrong,Jana Schaich Borg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，主张在高风险决策场景中，AI系统需要实现"认知对齐"（cognitive alignment），即以与用户相似的推理方式进行推理并忠实传达其推理过程，以确保用户能够理解和信任AI系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统越来越多地作为决策辅助工具、决策委托者或自主决策者被使用。然而，现有AI系统在推理方式上与用户不一致，导致用户难以理解和信任AI的决策过程，尤其是在高风险场景中，这可能成为AI采用的主要障碍。

Method: 作者通过三方面进行论证：(1) 综述认知对齐如何提升可理解性和可信赖性的证据；(2) 提供新的调查数据，显示许多用户在AI推理理由对他们重要时认为认知对齐是"必要的"；(3) 分析现有对齐方法与认知对齐目标之间的差距，并提出研究议程。

Result: 调查数据显示，许多用户当AI的推理依据对其重要时，认为认知对齐是"必要的"。同时，现有对齐方法（如基于人类反馈的强化学习等）与实现真正认知对齐之间存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 认知失调可能是AI在许多预期应用中采用的主要障碍。解决认知对齐问题对于创建用户既愿意信赖又有理由信赖的AI系统至关重要。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly employed as decision aids, decision delegates, or autonomous decision-makers. This position paper argues that in many settings, particularly high-stakes decision-making, we need accurate cognitively-aligned AI systems that reason similarly to their users, and faithfully communicate their reasoning. We review evidence that cognitive alignment improves understandability and trustworthiness, and provide new survey data showing that many users find cognitive alignment "essential" when an AI's rationale for a judgment or action is important to them. We outline the gaps between existing alignment methods and what is needed to achieve cognitive alignment, and present a research agenda to address these gaps. We argue that cognitive misalignment represents a likely impediment to AI adoption in many envisioned applications, and that addressing it is important for creating AI systems on which users are both willing and justified to rely.

</details>


### [190] [Don't Want Your LLM to Recommend Nuclear Strike? Try Asking It in Japanese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12373)
*Rian Touchent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究测试了六个提供商的九个大语言模型，发现提示语言（尤其是日语）可以显著改变模型在高风险战略场景中的决策——Claude Sonnet 4.6在使用日语提示时发射率从40%降至0%（不必要打击场景）和从93%降至17%（有争议场景），Gemini Pro 3.1从53%降至13%。通过跨语言实验隔离出机制：语言效应由模型被要求使用的推理语言驱动，而非输入语言本身。其余五个模型无语言效应，但在几乎所有条件下都会发射。这表明仅用英语评估LLM安全行为会遗漏其他语言中编码的风险与保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地用于战略和咨询场景，但其安全对齐通常仅用英语评估。作者质疑仅用英语评估是否足够，以及提示语言能否改变模型在高风险场景中的决策。

Method: 使用单轮博弈论场景（vignette），让模型为拥有核武器的国家就是否打击无防御对手提供建议。提示故意不涉及道德且跨语言战略上完全相同。测试九个模型、六个提供商。并设计跨语言实验隔离机制：在英文提示中指示模型用日语推理，对比语言效应来源。

Result: 日语提示显著降低Claude模型族的发射率（Sonnet 4.6从不必要打击的40%降至0%，有争议场景从93%降至17%），Gemini Pro 3.1从53%降至13%。跨语言实验表明，当指示模型用日语推理时发射率从93%降至37%，证明是推理语言而非输入语言驱动效应。用日语推理时模型自发生成提示中完全不存在的道德词汇（"moral cost"、"millions of lives"）。其余五个模型无语言效应，但几乎在所有条件下都选择发射。该效应需要模型在英语中已存在犹豫。

Conclusion: LLM安全行为具有语言依赖性，仅用英语评估会遗漏其他语言中编码的风险和保障。语言效应需要模型自身已在英语中表现出犹豫才可触发，这提示安全对齐的评估需要扩展到多语言场景。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used in strategic and advisory contexts, yet their safety alignment is typically evaluated in English only. We test nine models from six providers and ask whether the language of a prompt can change a model's decision in a high-stakes scenario. We use single-turn game-theoretic vignettes in which a model advises a nuclear-armed nation on whether to strike a defenseless opponent. The prompt is intentionally amoral and strategically identical across languages. We find that Japanese prompts reduce launch rates in the Claude model family: Claude Sonnet 4.6 drops from 40% to 0% in scenarios where the strike is unnecessary and from 93% to 17% in contested scenarios, with minimal effect when the strike is strategically rational. The effect extends to Gemini Pro 3.1 (53% to 13%). A cross-language experiment isolates the mechanism: when instructed to reason in Japanese in an English prompt, launch rates drop from 93% to 37%. It is the language the model is asked to reason in, not the language of the input, that drives the effect. When reasoning in Japanese, models spontaneously generate moral vocabulary (''moral cost'', ''millions of lives'') that is entirely absent from the prompt. Five other models show no language effect, but they launch in nearly every condition regardless of language. The effect requires a model that already hesitates in English. These results show that LLM safety behavior is language-dependent, and that evaluating in English alone can miss both risks and safeguards encoded in other languages.

</details>


### [191] [Dual-Flow Transformers: Decoupling the Primary Prefill Path from Additional Decode Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12385)
*Liming Liu,Mingze Wang,Tuo Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Dual-Flow Transformer，一种将推理计算按阶段分配的新架构。主流是完整的因果语言模型，负责处理prompt并写入KV缓存；辅助流仅在prompt处理完成后激活，仅用于续写预测，不写入持久状态或影响主流。两个流共享注意力、MLP和输出矩阵，仅使用独立的token嵌入和轻量耦合。在MoE模型中，该分离使得主/辅助专家扇出可独立控制，揭示了prefill-decode质量权衡，并展示了阶段特定专家分配的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在服务大量请求时，累积推理成本日益重要。两个推理阶段（prompt prefill和自回归decode）对硬件压力不同：prefill是并行且计算密集型，decode是串行且内存带宽密集型。传统的宽度或深度扩展会同时增加两个阶段的成本，因为每一层都必须在两个阶段中被计算。作者希望将额外的学习计算仅分配到续写预测阶段，同时保持prompt-wide的主计算和单一持久的KV缓存，从而允许分别控制两个阶段的成本与质量。

Method: Dual-Flow Transformer包含两个流：主流（primary flow）是完整的因果语言模型，处理prompt并写入KV缓存；辅助流（auxiliary flow）在prompt处理期间被省略，仅在prompt的最后一个位置起激活，为续写预测添加计算而不写入持久状态或影响主流。两个流共享主要的注意力和MLP矩阵以及输出矩阵，但使用独立的token嵌入和轻量耦合。权重共享和主缓存共享使得在分组执行期间可以复用加载的权重和缓存的键值。

Result: 在匹配token数的对比中，Dual-Flow在多种架构和数据配置下实现了更低的验证损失。在MoE模型中，流分离使得主/辅助专家扇出成为prefill成本、续写成本和预测质量的独立控制旋钮。实验研究了两种设置：（1）在固定prefill专家计算下增加decode计算；（2）在固定的decode专家预算中重分配两个流。这些实验揭示了prefill-decode质量权衡，并展示了阶段特定专家分配的潜力。

Conclusion: Dual-Flow证明了对推理计算进行阶段特定分配是可行的：通过将额外计算仅注入decode阶段而不增加prefill成本，可以在相同token预算下获得更低的验证损失。MoE场景下阶段特定专家分配提供了对推理成本和预测质量更细粒度的控制，打开了一个新的优化维度。

Abstract: As large language models serve more requests, cumulative inference cost is becoming increasingly important relative to one-time training cost. The two inference phases stress hardware differently: prompt prefill is parallel and typically compute-bound, whereas autoregressive decode is sequential and often memory-bandwidth-bound. Conventional width or depth scaling increases both costs together because every added layer is evaluated in both phases. We ask whether additional learned computation can instead be allocated to continuation prediction while preserving the prompt-wide primary computation and a single persistent key-value (KV) cache. We introduce the Dual-Flow Transformer. Its primary flow is a complete causal language model that processes the prompt and writes the KV cache. The auxiliary flow is omitted during prompt processing and activated only from the final prompt position onward, adding continuation-prediction computation without writing persistent state or influencing the primary flow. The two flows share major attention, MLP, and output matrices, while using separate token embeddings and lightweight coupling. Sharing weights and the primary cache also creates opportunities to reuse loaded weights and cached keys and values during grouped execution. Across matched-token comparisons, Dual-Flow achieves lower validation loss across architectures and data configurations. In MoE models, the separation makes primary and auxiliary expert fan-outs independent controls over prompt cost, continuation cost, and predictive quality. We study two regimes: increasing decode computation at fixed prefill expert computation, and reallocating a fixed decode expert budget between the two flows. These experiments expose a prefill-decode-quality trade-off and demonstrate the potential of phase-specific expert allocation.

</details>


### [192] [Learning to Adapt Cross-Domain Preferences via Meta-LoRA for LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12389)
*Xuefei Wang,Jun Han,Zixuan Wang,Qingkai Zeng,Xiao Wang,Ruijie Wang,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PAC-Bayes正则化的Meta-LoRA方法用于跨域零样本/少样本个性化，通过元学习的LoRA初始化控制更新强度以避免过拟合，并将个性化先验分解为用户和域组件，在多个基准上显著超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨域零样本或少样本个性化旨在仅凭少量目标域交互生成用户偏好的回复。现有适应方法在证据稀疏时难以校准更新幅度导致过拟合，而历史迁移方法常将用户偏好与源域伪影纠缠，产生不可靠的个性化先验并导致负迁移。

Method: 提出PAC-Bayes正则化的Meta-LoRA：将元学习的LoRA初始化同时作为适应起点和先验中心，根据支撑集大小和预测不确定性调整更新强度，限制稀疏或模糊证据下的过拟合；同时将个性化先验功能性地分解为用户组件（可读提示）和域组件（保留拓扑结构的软令牌），分别用于稳定偏好表达和域特定隐空间条件化。

Result: 在多个基准和个性化任务上一致超越强基线。在HiCUPID上，相比最优竞争基线，跨域胜率退化降低47.9%，未见用户冷启动场景下胜率提升110.2%。

Conclusion: 通过证据质量感知的适应控制与先验的功能性分解，可有效避免过拟合与负迁移，实现跨域个性化性能的显著提升。

Abstract: Cross-domain zero- or few-shot personalization aims to generate user-preferred responses in unseen conversational domains from only a handful of target-domain interactions. Existing adaptation methods struggle to calibrate update magnitude under sparse evidence and thus overfit, whereas history-transfer methods often entangle user preferences with source-domain artifacts, yielding unreliable personalization priors and negative transfer. To calibrate adaptation to evidence quality, we propose PAC-Bayes-regularized Meta-LoRA, which uses a meta-learned LoRA initialization as both the adaptation start and prior center, while adjusting update strength according to support-set size and predictive uncertainty. This limits overfitting under sparse or ambiguous evidence while permitting stronger personalization as evidence grows. Controlled adaptation alone does not determine which preferences should transfer across domains or how they should be expressed. We therefore functionally decompose personalization priors into user and domain components, using a human-readable prompt for stable preferences and topology-preserving soft tokens for domain-specific hidden-space conditioning. Experiments across multiple benchmarks and personalization tasks show consistent gains over strong baselines. On HiCUPID, our method reduces cross-domain win-rate degradation by 47.9% relative to the best competing baseline and improves win rate by 110.2% under unseen-user cold start.

</details>


### [193] [Research Assistant: AstraZeneca's Agentic System for R&D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12395)
*Piotr Grabowski,Mohamed Alameen,Jorge Bretones,Sabina Cardell,Miguel Carmona,Gavin Edwards,Ben Grainger,Sameh Hassan,Erik Jansson,Artur Kuziakhmetov,Albert Maristany,Hebatallah Mohamed,Andriy Nikolov,Sebastian Nilsson,Mark O'Donoghue,James Pacileo,Ashiq Sultan,Alex Voegele,Michael Ughetto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 介绍 AstraZeneca 内部开发的基于 LLM 的研究助手系统，为科学家和临床医生提供跨多数据源的聊天式界面，支持快速问答和复杂多步研究任务，响应基于检索证据并链接原始来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帮助 AstraZeneca 的科学家和临床医生高效探索生物医学问题，整合分散在不同数据源中的信息（文献、知识图谱、化学、临床试验、安全性资源、表达数据和内部实验系统），以支持日常研发工作流。

Method: 构建基于 LLM 的 Research Assistant 系统，提供聊天式接口，集成多种数据源；设计快速模式用于直接问答，多步模式用于复杂研究任务；采用检索增强生成（RAG）确保响应基于证据并链接回原始来源。

Result: 该系统已在大规模部署中用于支持 AstraZeneca 的日常研发工作流，本文概述了系统架构、关键产品设计决策以及大规模部署中的经验教训。

Conclusion: Research Assistant 展示了内部 LLM 系统在制药研发场景中整合多源证据、提供可追溯的问答能力的可行性，其部署经验为类似企业级 LLM 产品提供了有价值的参考。

Abstract: We describe Research Assistant, an internal LLM-based system developed at AstraZeneca to help scientists and clinicians explore biomedical questions across a broad range of data sources. The system provides a chat-style interface that brings together evidence from scientific literature, knowledge graphs, chemistry, clinical trials, safety resources, expression data, and internal experimental systems. It supports both a fast mode for direct question answering and a multi-step mode for more complex research tasks. Responses are grounded in retrieved evidence and linked back to the original sources, allowing users to review and further explore the underlying data. In this technical note, we outline the system architecture, the main design choices behind the product, and lessons learned from deploying it at scale to support day-to-day R&D workflows across AstraZeneca.

</details>


### [194] [Large Language Models Can Follow Instructions, But Not Many at Once: Phase Transitions in Compositional Constraint Satisfaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12426)
*Mariya I. Vasileva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出约束饱和评估（CSE）基准，系统研究大语言模型在同时满足多个显式约束时的性能退化规律。通过对15个模型、36种约束类型在k=1-12个约束条件下的369,753次检查发现：单约束通过率虽缓慢下降，但全约束同时满足的联合成功率急剧坍塌；结构类约束比词汇类约束退化更快；失败几乎独立导致累积呈乘法效应；可靠指令跟随在5-6个约束后崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型已广泛部署于需要同时满足多个显式约束的场景（如推理结构、安全边界、输出格式），但现有研究仅关注单一约束的处理能力，对组合约束场景（多约束需同时成立）缺乏系统刻画。作者旨在回答三个问题：性能如何随约束数量快速退化？退化由什么因素主导？能否缓解这种崩塌？

Method: 作者提出约束饱和评估（CSE）基准：通过程序化生成方式系统变化同时约束的数量k（k=1-12），每个约束均由确定性规则验证器评分，完全排除LLM作为裁判的偏差。包含15个模型、36种约束类型，共369,753次检查。通过对比单约束通过率与联合成功概率，分析不同类型约束（结构类vs词汇类）的退化差异，并通过故障独立性检验揭示累积效应的机制。

Result: 三项核心发现：(1) 单约束通过率随k增加呈渐进可预测的衰减，但全部约束同时满足的概率急剧坍塌——在k=8时，模型单约束通过率约41%，但全部8个约束同时成功的概率仅5.7%；(2) 约束退化不均匀：每增加一个约束，结构类约束的基线能力损失是词汇类约束的2倍，这源于需持续追踪的约束与免于组合影响的二元决策之间的理解维持差距；(3) 失败几乎相互独立，这正是累积呈乘法效应的原因；残余耦合追踪共享输出特征而非成对干扰。最强模型在7个约束时探针级成功率跌破50%，12/15个模型在3个或更少约束时即失效。

Conclusion: 可靠指令跟随在同时5-6个约束时系统性崩溃，为LLM的组合约束处理能力设定了量化边界。研究揭示了退化主要由约束类型特征（结构维持vs二元判断）与失败的准独立性驱动，为理解LLM多约束遵循的失效机制和设计缓解策略提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in settings that require simultaneous adherence to multiple explicit constraints - reasoning structure, safety boundaries, output schemas. Individual constraints are handled proficiently, but the compositional regime, where many must hold jointly, remains poorly characterized: how rapidly does performance degrade, what governs the degradation, and can the collapse be mitigated? We introduce Constraint Saturation Evaluation (CSE), a procedurally generated benchmark that systematically varies the number of simultaneous constraints (k), with every constraint scored by a deterministic, rule-based verifier and zero LLM-judge involvement: 15 models, 36 constraint types, 369,753 checks at k=1-12. Three findings emerge. First, per-constraint pass rate decays gradually and predictably, while the chance of satisfying all k constraints collapses - a model passing individual constraints at ~41% at k=8 succeeds on all eight just 5.7% of the time. Second, constraints do not degrade equally: structural constraints lose 2x more baseline capability per added constraint than lexical ones, ordered by a comprehension-maintenance gap that separates constraints requiring sustained tracking from binary decisions immune to composition. Third, failures are nearly independent, which is what makes the accumulation multiplicative; the residual coupling that does exist tracks shared output features rather than pairwise interference - a wrong sentence count fails every constraint that reads it. Reliable instruction following breaks down beyond 5-6 simultaneous constraints: probe-level success falls below 50% at 7 constraints for the strongest model, and at 3 or fewer for 12 of 15.

</details>


### [195] [MindMemOS: A Portable and Self-Evolving Memory Operating Layer for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12428)
*Kaichao Liang,Yuqi Cui,Hao Kong,Xinyuan Huang,Guohaotian Hou,Qingcan Kang,Liang Chen,Yiyang Yin,Ke Ye,Jiaquan Guo,Da Chen,Lingan Zeng,Yixing Peng,Rong Yao,Shixiong Kai,Mingxuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MindMemOS 是一个可移植、自进化的记忆操作系统层，通过统一实体-属性-时间结构组织开放世界信息，支持场景自适应记忆建模、高阶模式发现、自主记忆精炼和持续技能进化，在多个基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理的记忆系统在开发后通常固定不变，缺乏根据持续使用调整记忆模型、组织策略和程序性知识的能力，这限制了代理在长期交互中的适应性和个性化维持。

Method: MindMemOS提出统一实体属性时间结构组织信息，包含三个核心组件：(1) MindMemEvolve算法通过验证驱动的进化搜索优化目标场景的记忆模式，并通过"梦境"机制合并冗余记录、解决冲突；(2) 隐式纠正反馈作为人在环路信号识别和修正不准确或不对齐的记忆；(3) MindSkillEvolve算法将代理执行轨迹转化为可复用且渐进精炼的技能。

Result: MindMemOS在LOCOMO上达到94.03%准确率，在PersonaMem上达到70.63%准确率。MindSkillEvolve相比初始技能基线在SpreadsheetBench任务上提升了9.2个百分点。

Conclusion: MindMemOS通过自进化的记忆系统架构有效解决了AI代理记忆中动态适应的难题，实验证明其记忆组织与技能进化机制能显著提升代理在长期交互场景中的性能。

Abstract: Memory is a core component of AI agents, enabling them to accumulate experience, maintain personalization, and adapt over long-term interactions. However, existing memory systems often remain fixed after development, limiting their ability to adapt their memory models, organization strategies, and procedural knowledge through continued use. We present MindMemOS, a portable and self-evolving memory operating layer that organizes open-world information using a unified entity property timestructure. MindMemOS supports scenario-adaptive memory modeling, higher-order pattern discovery, autonomous memory refinement, and continuous skill evolution. Its MindMemEvolve algorithm employs validation-driven evolutionary search to optimize memory schemas for target scenarios, whiledreaming consolidates accumulated memories by merging redundant records and resolving conflicts. In addition, implicit corrective feedback serves as a human-in-the-loop signal for identifying and revising potentially inaccurate or misaligned memories. Its MindSkillEvolve algorithm further transforms agent execution trajectories into reusable and progressively refined skills. MindMemOS achieves 94.03% accuracy on LOCOMO and 70.63% on PersonaMem. MindSkillEvolve improves SpreadsheetBench success by 9.2 percentage points over the initial-skill baseline.

</details>


### [196] [Governed Persistent Memory: Source-Bound State Semantics and Fail-Closed Release for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12476)
*Guodong Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Governed Persistent Memory (GPM)，一种可审计的双时态状态转换模型，用于解决智能体长期记忆中陈旧、矛盾或被撤销记录可能支持错误输出的问题。在3,600例基准测试中实现全部匹配，在2,400集群密封评测中达到2,400/2,400的正确率（对照组仅600/2,400），并通过有限状态模型和差分测试验证了契约完备性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统智能体长期记忆采用"选择-存储-检索"的简单范式，但检索过程并不判断记录是否互相矛盾、已被取代、撤销、删除或过期。这些过期或无效记录可能会被用于支撑对外输出，从而导致错误或误导性结论。现有方法缺乏对记忆生命周期状态的显式管理和可审计约束。

Method: GPM采用可审计的双时态（bitemporal）状态转换模型，包括四个核心机制：(1) 源绑定准入（source-bound admission）；(2) 派生的生命周期状态（derived lifecycle state）；(3) 当前公开屏障（current public barriers）；(4) 失败关闭的结构化发布（fail-closed structured release）。模型由五条可执行子句约束：账本完整性、源绑定、冲突隔离、撤销/删除后不复活、以及在一个已验证头的新鲜视图上进行精确声明闭合。验证方法包括hash冻结的3,600例基准测试、密封端到端服务评测、331,776语义状态和1,990,656查询状态的有限模型验证，以及100,000条轨迹的三引擎差分测试。

Result: 在GPM-ReleaseBench的3,600例测试中，GPM完成全部输出匹配；最优的简化策略仅匹配1,800/3,600，并在50%的违规案例上产生未匹配发布。密封端到端评测V3中，受约束通道在2,400/2,400集群上正确（对照组本地Qwen2.5-7B仅600/2,400），修复全部1,800个基线失败且无回归（单侧95%下界99.875%和99.834%）。V5重新密封（中英文命令分支，含生成日期固定）再次获得每分支2,400/2,400。有限模型无全契约反例，三引擎差分测试零不匹配。

Conclusion: GPM提供了对智能体记忆治理的一种有界契约和实现层面的保证，能有效防止冲突、过期、已撤销或已删除记录泄漏到对外输出中。但作者明确限定：结果仅证明契约和实现正确性，而非开放世界下的模型准确率或世界真实性；受治理答案是确定性的服务输出，7B结果仅为对照组比较，并非宣称语言模型本身达到完美准确率。

Abstract: Long-term agent memory is usually treated as select--store--retrieve, but retrieval does not decide whether contradictory, superseded, retracted, deleted, or stale records may support an outgoing claim. We introduce Governed Persistent Memory (GPM), an auditable bitemporal state-transition model with source-bound admission, derived lifecycle state, current public barriers, and fail-closed structured release. Five executable clauses cover ledger integrity, source binding, conflict isolation, non-revival after retraction or deletion, and exact claim closure over a fresh view at one verified head.
  On a prespecified hash-frozen 3,600-case GPM-ReleaseBench, GPM matches all complete outcomes; the strongest of three intentionally simple complete policies matches 1,800/3,600 and makes unmatched releases on 50% of violation cases. A separate sealed end-to-end service evaluation exercises real ingestion and release across eight query families. In its publicly disclosed V3 arm, the governed lane is correct on 2,400/2,400 clusters versus 600/2,400 for ungoverned local Qwen2.5-7B; it repairs all 1,800 baseline failures with no regression (one-sided 95% lower bounds 99.875% and 99.834%). A later V5 reseal over Chinese- and English-command arms, with generation-date pinning and no post-freeze reducer amendment, again obtains 2,400/2,400 per arm. A production-code-independent finite model explores 331,776 semantic and 1,990,656 query states without a full-contract counterexample, and a 100,000-trace three-engine differential yields zero mismatches.
  These are bounded contract and implementation results, not open-world model accuracy or evidence of world truth. Governed answers in the sealed service evaluation are deterministic service outputs; the 7B result is the ungoverned comparison, not a claim that a language model itself became perfectly accurate.

</details>


### [197] [$\varepsilon$-MemEvo: Adaptive Cross-Task Memory Transfer for LLM Program Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12522)
*Aofan Liu,Shiyuan Song,Yiyan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ε-MemEvo是一个用于LLM程序演化的跨任务知识迁移框架，通过存储任务无关的策略记忆（而非原始代码）并在自适应注入门控的控制下将其注入新任务的搜索过程，在8个优化基准上以不到1%的计算开销实现了平均+8.7%的AUCC相对提升和+9.4%的早期收敛改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的程序演化系统（如FunSearch、AlphaEvolve）在每次任务中是独立优化的，搜索完成后经验即被丢弃，无法实现跨任务的知识积累和迁移，造成计算资源的浪费。

Method: ε-MemEvo将先前搜索经验以任务无关的策略记忆（紧凑的自然语言摘要，记录成功的算法策略而非原始代码）形式存储；采用自适应注入门控机制，根据检索到的记忆与当前任务的语义匹配度决定是否注入以及注入强度，以避免负迁移。

Result: 在8个跨数学优化和系统工程领域的基准上，基于GPT-5主干，ε-MemEvo在所有8个任务上均优于AdaEvolve，平均相对AUCC提升+8.7%，早期阶段收敛提升+9.4%；消融实验表明朴素记忆注入可能灾难性失败，而自适应门控在全部5个消融任务中保持安全；数据更新的后验可解释，在搜索改进阶段偏好跳过，在早期和晚期平台期从跳过转向提示；计算开销小于1%。

Conclusion: ε-MemEvo验证了任务无关的策略记忆加自适应注入门控可实现安全、有效的跨任务知识迁移，同时保持极低的计算开销，为LLM程序演化系统的经验积累与迁移提供了一种可行范式。

Abstract: LLM-based program evolution systems such as FunSearch and AlphaEvolve have shown strong ability to discover novel algorithms, but typically optimize each task in isolation, discarding search experience after completion. We introduce $\varepsilon$-MemEvo, a framework for cross-task knowledge transfer in LLM program evolution. $\varepsilon$-MemEvo stores prior experience as task-agnostic tactic memories: compact natural-language summaries of successful algorithmic strategies rather than raw code, enabling transfer across tasks with different APIs and evaluators. To avoid negative transfer from semantically mismatched memories, $\varepsilon$-MemEvo uses an adaptive injection gate that decides whether retrieved memories should be injected, and at what intensity. We evaluate $\varepsilon$-MemEvo on 8 diverse optimization benchmarks spanning mathematical optimization and systems engineering, using a content-level Leave-One-Out protocol that excludes target-task memory entries. On the primary GPT-5 backbone, $\varepsilon$-MemEvo improves AUCC over AdaEvolve on all 8 tasks, with a mean relative gain of +8.7%, and improves early-stage convergence by +9.4% on average. Ablations show that naive memory injection can fail catastrophically, while adaptive gating remains safe across all five ablation tasks. The data-updated posterior is interpretable in observed states: it favors skip during improving search and shifts from skip to hint across early and late plateaus. These gains incur less than 1% computational overhead.

</details>


### [198] [CAS: A Causal Attribution Score for Local and Global Explainable Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12555)
*Michael Georgiades,Charalambia Varnava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出因果归因分数（CAS），一种以干预效应为目标的因果解释框架。CAS基于识别的干预联盟博弈，通过因果Shapley贡献分配联合干预对比，并转换为局部和全局CAS摘要。与传统预测解释方法（如SHAP）不同，CAS区分"预测结果"与"解释因果效应异质性"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预测性解释方法（如SHAP）只能归因模型输出，并不能归因干预对真实世界结果的影响。这导致在因果推断场景中，决策者无法区分哪些特征预测了结果、哪些特征真正调节了因果效应。需要一种能够直接归因干预效应的解释框架。

Method: CAS首先从识别的干预联盟博弈出发，利用因果Shapley贡献分配联合干预对比（而非简单的逐项归一化），然后将原始结果尺度的效应转换为三种输出：局部CAS、符号局部CAS和两个互补的全局CAS摘要（包括Feature-CAS，用于衡量治疗效应修饰变量的重要性）。

Result: 在已知真实值的基准模拟中（8次重复主效应交互模拟，n=2200，3个动作），联盟感知CAS的平均局部MAE为0.107，优于逐项归一化的0.173和全局归一化绝对ATE向量的0.213。与逐项归一化相比，配对优势从可加性下的-0.003增加到强交互下的0.091。在两个实证DoubleML数据集（401(k)资格/净资产n=9915；宾州再就业奖金/失业时长n=5099）中，预测SHAP/TreeSHAP排名与Feature-CAS治疗效应修饰者排名存在实质性差异。宾州数据中dep1从预测全局排名13升至Feature-CAS排名2，并成为领先的局部Feature-CAS修饰者。

Conclusion: CAS的价值在于解耦"预测结果"与"解释因果效应异质性"这两个不同的解释目标。通过显式的干预目标和联盟感知的因果归因架构，CAS在交互场景下显著优于传统的归一化方法，并能揭示预测解释方法遗漏的治疗效应修饰特征，为因果解释提供了新的报告层。

Abstract: Predictive explanation methods attribute a model output; they do not, by themselves, attribute an intervention effect on the real-world outcome. We introduce the Causal Attribution Score (CAS), a compact score architecture for causal explanation. CAS starts from an identified interventional coalition game, allocates the joint intervention contrast with causal Shapley contributions, and converts those raw outcome-scale effects into Local CAS, Signed Local CAS, and two complementary Global CAS summaries. The innovation is not a new Shapley formula, but a local-to-global causal reporting layer with an explicit intervention target. In the known-truth benchmark, eight repeated primary-interaction simulations (n = 2,200 each, three actions) gave mean Local CAS MAE of 0.107 for coalition-aware CAS, compared with 0.173 for one-at-a-time normalisation and 0.213 for a global normalised absolute ATE vector. The paired advantage over one-at-a-time normalisation increased from -0.003 under additivity to 0.091 under strong interactions. On both empirical DoubleML datasets, 401(k) eligibility/net financial assets (n = 9,915) and Pennsylvania reemployment bonus/unemployment duration (n = 5,099), predictive SHAP/TreeSHAP rankings differed materially from Feature-CAS rankings of treatment-effect modifiers. In Pennsylvania, dep1 (exactly one dependent) moved from predictive global rank 13 to Feature-CAS rank 2 and was the leading local Feature-CAS modifier. These results isolate the added value of separating what predicts the outcome from what explains heterogeneity in an estimated causal effect.

</details>


### [199] [Trie Automata for Constrained Decoding over Large Finite Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12574)
*Xingzi Xu,Karim Bouyarmane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出trie自动机，一种利用Aho-Corasick多模式匹配为有限字符串集合预计算token掩码的专门化约束解码机制，相比通用的XGrammar后端在单步计算上快7倍，在批量服务中实现29倍端到端吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在生成结构化输出时需要满足预定义模式（如从有限集合中选择合法字符串），但现有的通用语法编译方法在处理数千个合法值时会遇到"基数墙"（cardinality wall），性能急剧下降，无法满足大规模场景的需求。

Method: 提出trie自动机，利用有限集合的特性（共享前缀、有界深度、已知基数），通过Aho-Corasick多模式匹配预先计算每个节点的token掩码。预计算掩码实现了无状态的推理路径，绕过了传统的guided decoding流水线。

Result: 与XGrammar相比，单步合法token计算快7倍（0.65μs vs 5.8μs）；在K≥300时编译快2-6.5倍；在batch size为256时，vLLM端到端吞吐达219 req/s vs XGrammar的7.5 req/s（29倍提升）；在7种tokenizer家族（32K-262K词表）上，K=10,000时仍保持亚100ms编译时间，单步成本与集合大小无关，且保证100%输出有效性。

Conclusion: trie自动机通过利用有限集合的结构特性进行预计算，有效突破了约束解码的基数墙，在编译速度、单步计算效率和端到端批量服务吞吐方面均实现了显著提升，为大语言模型的高效结构化生成提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly need to generate structured outputs that conform to predefined schemas, with one common constraint being selection from a finite set of valid strings. Current constrained decoding systems handle this through general-purpose grammar compilation, which becomes prohibitively slow as the number of valid values grows into the thousands, a cardinality wall. We introduce the trie automaton, a specialized mechanism that exploits finite-set structure (shared prefixes, bounded depth, known cardinality) via Aho-Corasick multi-pattern matching to precompute per-node token masks. The trie achieves 7X faster per-step valid-token computation (0.65 us vs. 5.8 us) compared to XGrammar, one of the primary backends in vLLM and SGLang, and 2--6.5X faster compilation at K >= 300. Because precomputed masks enable a stateless serving path that bypasses the guided decoding pipeline, this advantage compounds in batch serving: end-to-end vLLM throughput reaches 219 req/s vs. XGrammar's 7.5 req/s at batch size 256 (29X). The 29X combines the algorithmic speedup with integration-path savings that only precomputed masks can unlock. Across seven tokenizer families (32K--262K vocabulary), the trie maintains sub-100ms compilation up to K = 10,000 and flat per-step cost regardless of set size, while guaranteeing 100% output validity.

</details>


### [200] [Reasoning Jury: Multi-Model Consensus for Evaluating Reasoning Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12585)
*Congchao Wang,Diwakar Singh,Qiaozi Gao,Spyros Matsoukas,Yang Liu,Mahdi Namazifar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Reasoning Jury系统，用多个开源LLM组成的陪审团加共识机制替代单一评判模型，用于识别推理模型的长推理轨迹中的缺陷。该系统通过陪审员间相互评判和主持人主持的讨论达成共识，在准确性上显著超越前沿模型（opus-4.6、sonnet-4.6、gemini-3.1-pro），且成本仅为前沿模型的8-15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理LLM质量的提升需要高质量的长推理轨迹评判能力，用于数据筛选、强化学习训练信号和模型性能评估。然而，单一模型评判（即使是前沿模型）在识别长推理轨迹缺陷方面表现不佳；同时，在线训练推理LLM时通常因使用条款限制而无法使用前沿模型。因此需要一个既可靠又合规的评判机制。

Method: Reasoning Jury系统采用"陪审团"而非"单一评判者"的架构：由多个LLM组成陪审团，通过主持人（moderator）组织陪审员之间的讨论与相互批评，陪审员可基于讨论修改初始投票。主持人通过陪审员间的审议（deliberation）或判断整合（consolidation）达成共识，最终识别推理轨迹中的缺陷及其严重程度。

Result: 使用开源模型（如gpt-oss-120b）组成的陪审团在正确识别推理缺陷方面显著优于前沿模型（opus-4.6、sonnet-4.6、gemini-3.1-pro）。此外，陪审团的综合成本（初始评判、讨论、整合等）仅为前沿模型LLM-as-a-judge设置成本的8%-15%。

Conclusion: Reasoning Jury证明了"多模型陪审团+协商共识"的评判范式在推理缺陷识别上优于单一大模型评判，同时兼具成本效益和合规性。这些评判还能用于深入理解推理LLM在基准测试中的失败模式，为模型性能分析提供更丰富的洞察。

Abstract: Improving reasoning LLMs requires the ability to judge the quality of long reasoning traces for effective reasoning data curation, strong training signals during reinforcement learning, and an in-depth understanding of reasoning behaviors during model performance evaluation. Additionally, surfacing reasoning mistakes that the model makes would enable improving the model's performance at runtime through providing feedback. Due to the difficulty of this complex task on long reasoning traces, single-model judges (even frontier models) do not do well at identifying reasoning defects. Additionally, leveraging frontier models during online training of reasoning LLMs is generally prohibited due to guardrails in terms of use. In this work, we introduce Reasoning Jury, a system that replaces the single judge with a jury of LLMs and a moderated consensus mechanism, to improve the fidelity of judgments for identifying reasoning defects. In reasoning jury, defects of a reasoning trace and their severity are surfaced through a deliberation where a moderator conducts a discussion amongst the jury where the jurors critique each other's judgments and get to modify their initial votes. The moderator derives a consensus through deliberation amongst jurors or consolidation of judgements. We show that Reasoning Jury with a jury of open-weight models (e.g., gpt-oss-120b) is able to significantly outperform frontier models (opus-4.6, sonnet-4.6, and gemini-3.1-pro) at correctly identifying reasoning defects. Besides accuracy performance improvements, the aggregated cost of the jury (initial verdicts, deliberations, consolidation, etc.) is a fraction (8 to 15%) of the cost of running frontier models in LLM-as-a-judge setup. We also show how these judgements can be leveraged to understand failure modes of reasoning LLMs on benchmarks, which allows much deeper understanding of a model's performance.

</details>


### [201] [Auditable agentic AI for evidence-grounded thyroid ultrasound diagnosis and reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12590)
*Haifan Gong,Shiyu Chen,Bodong Wang,Yuqi Wang,Shijie Wang,Guoliang You,Xinyu Xiong,Haowei Wang,Mingzhi Mao,Dexing Kong,Qinghua Liu,Wei Lou,Fei Chen,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ThyroidXAgent，一个临床医生可交互的智能体AI系统，协调甲状腺超声诊断的多个专项工具（结节分割、定位、风险分层、良恶性分类、淋巴结转移预测、报告生成），并通过可审计的病例级证据记录支持临床审阅。基于约30万张超声图像和2.4万配对报告的多中心数据集OpenThyroidDB开发，在28,458例非重叠测试集上评估，分割Dice达87.21%，良恶性分类AUROC达0.9466，并将医生报告诊断一致性从70.3%提升至86.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甲状腺超声诊断需要协调病变定位、测量、风险分层和报告生成等多个环节，但现有AI系统大多孤立地处理单一任务，缺乏对临床审阅流程的支持。亟需一个集成多任务、可审计且允许临床医生干预修正的智能体系统。

Method: 构建OptenThyroidDB多中心多任务数据集（约30万张图像+2.4万配对报告）；设计ThyroidXAgent智能体架构，协调多个专项诊断工具并将输出存储为可审计的病例级证据记录；引入ThyClinScore病变级临床语义评估指标用于报告生成质量评估。

Result: 平均分割Dice 87.21%；良恶性分类AUROC 0.9466；淋巴结转移预测AUROC 0.864；滤泡性vs乳头状癌分类AUROC 0.805；证据驱动的报告组装优于多模态语言模型基线；医生分类准确率提升，报告诊断一致性从70.3%升至86.2%，分割和报告时间分别减少35.9%和27.4%。

Conclusion: 研究证实了可审计、临床医生可修正的智能体AI在甲状腺超声诊断和报告生成中的有效性，为AI系统在临床工作流中的可信部署提供了支持。

Abstract: Thyroid ultrasound diagnosis requires coordinated lesion localization, measurement, risk stratification and reporting, yet most AI systems address these tasks in isolation and provide limited support for clinical review. We present ThyroidXAgent, a clinician-interactive agentic AI system that coordinates specialized diagnostic tools and stores their outputs as an auditable case-level evidence record. The system was developed using OpenThyroidDB, a multicentre, multitask resource integrating approximately 0.3 million ultrasound images and 24,000 paired reports, and was evaluated on 28,458 non-overlapping test cases, including 8,721 cases from 35 centres in the private NHC-MISD-TUS cohort. Across heterogeneous datasets, ThyroidXAgent achieved a mean Dice score of 87.21 percent for nodule segmentation and a mean AUROC of 0.9466 for benign-malignant classification. The same workflow supported lymph-node metastasis prediction and follicular versus papillary thyroid carcinoma classification, with AUROCs of 0.864 and 0.805, respectively. For report generation, evidence-grounded assembly outperformed multimodal language-model baselines across three cohorts. ThyClinScore, a lesion-level clinical semantic metric introduced here, showed the strongest correlation with a location-aware language-model judge. ThyroidXAgent improved physician classification accuracy, increased report diagnostic consistency from 70.3 percent to 86.2 percent, and reduced segmentation and reporting time by 35.9 percent and 27.4 percent, respectively. These findings support auditable, clinician-correctable agentic AI for thyroid ultrasound diagnosis and reporting.

</details>


### [202] [DiG-bench: Discovery in Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12593)
*Ruairidh M. Battleday,Kai Sandbrink,Jimi Cullen-Drohan,Zihan Yan,Timothy Muller,Clare Maguire,Ales Kubicek,Fraser Greenlee-Scott,Sukrit Sumant,Tri Dao,Jürgen Schmidhuber,Michal Valko,Joshua Tenenbaum,Thomas L. Griffiths,Zeb Kurth-Nelson,James C. R. Whittington*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发布了一个名为DiG-bench（Discovery in Games）的新基准测试，用于评估AI在受控环境中通过交互和实验发现新知识（即新规则）的能力。该基准包含70个独立游戏，每个游戏编码为短字符串并具有独特的变换规则，分7个难度层级，其中21个游戏公开、49个保密用于安全评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现——即提出新概括——是科学过程的核心部分。然而，当前AI基准测试领域存在空白：很少有基准直接测试AI在目标未知的受控环境中通过实验发现新知识的能力。

Method: DiG-bench包含70个独立游戏，每个游戏编码为短字符串并具有需要通过与环境的交互和实验来发现的独特变换规则。游戏关卡提供一系列挑战以测试规则是否被发现，且每个关卡的胜利条件也是未知的。游戏分为7个难度层级，最低层级可被多个模型常规解决，最高层级挑战最强agent模型的表现。

Result: 最基础的难度层级可被多个模型常规解决，而最高难度层级即使对最好的agent工具中的模型也构成挑战。所有70个游戏均至少有一位人类受试者首次尝试即可解决。21个游戏公开发布，其余49个保密用于安全评估。

Conclusion: DiG-bench填补了AI基准测试中关于科学发现能力的评估空白，提供了一个结构化、分层的测试环境，既能区分不同能力的模型，又能通过人类验证确保任务可解性，并通过保密子集防止基准污染。

Abstract: Discovery---formulating novel generalizations---is a central part of the scientific process. Despite its importance, there is a gap in the current AI benchmark landscape, with few benchmarks directly probing the capacity for discovering new knowledge with experimentation in controlled environments where the objective is unknown. To address this gap, we release a new benchmark: DiG-bench (Discovery in Games). DiG-bench consists of a set of 70 independent games. Each game is encoded as a short string and has unique transformation rules that must be discovered through interaction and experimentation. The levels of the game present a series of challenges to test whether the rules have been discovered, where the win conditions for each level are also unknown. We provide games at seven tiers of difficulty for AI agents. The lowest tier is routinely solvable by multiple models, while the highest tier challenges the best models in agentic harnesses. All 70 games were solved by at least one human on first attempt. A subset of 21 games is released publicly, and the remainder is held private for secure evaluation.

</details>


### [203] [Dead text or binding clause? Measuring and restoring constraint influence in black-box LLM dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12599)
*Haoyuan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SYSNAME系统，通过模型API解决多轮对话中用户撤销约束后模型仍执行已撤销约束（称为"行为复发"或"撤销惰性"）的问题。系统包括三部分：合约账本（对每个约束配可执行检查器并记录撤销墓碑）、顺序消融探针（测量逐子句遵守度和增量行为影响）、修复阶梯（在token和尝试匹配预算下修复）。实验在HumanEval多任务上验证了预编译显著减少复发、探针可预测复发、墓碑注释可恢复部分修复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮对话中用户可以像施加约束一样轻松撤销约束，但撤销往往不能可靠生效——模型仍会执行已被撤回的要求（有时甚至在声称已移除的注释下方）。现有工具无法按子句衡量这种影响、无法在交付前预测它、也无法在匹配预算下修复它，存在三个空白。

Method: SYSNAME通过模型API实现三个组件：(1) 合约账本——将每个约束与可执行检查器配对，将撤销记录为墓碑，并把净约束状态提前编译为单一规格；(2) 顺序消融探针——测量逐子句遵守度和增量行为效应；(3) 修复阶梯——在token和尝试匹配的预算下进行修复。

Result: 在HumanEval任务上：8B模型在约束负载增加时复发率从14.4%攀升至63.2%，更强模型处于基线水平；预编译显著减少复发（与无账本的验证器重试基线相比，p<0.05）；探针预测复发的AUROC为0.83；单句墓碑注释可恢复约三分之一的编译效果并通过安慰剂对照；交付开销为1.4倍，总API计算成本为2.1倍。

Conclusion: 通过合约账本、消融探针和修复阶梯，撤销失败从对话状态的不可见属性转变为可测量、可预测、可修复的属性，以可接受的交付开销和API计算成本实现。

Abstract: Multi-turn dialogues let users revoke constraints as easily as impose them, but revocation does not reliably take effect: models keep enacting withdrawn requirements (occasionally beneath comments asserting their removal), a failure we call \emph{behavioral relapse}, or revocation inertia. No existing instrument measures this influence per clause, predicts it before delivery, or repairs it under matched budgets. \sysname{} closes the three gaps through the model API alone: a contract ledger pairs every constraint with an executable checker, records revocations as tombstones, and compiles the net constraint state ahead of time into a single specification; a sequential ablation probe measures per-clause adherence and incremental behavioral effect; a repair ladder operates under token- and attempt-matched budgets. On \dataname{} (\NTasks{} HumanEval tasks, \NClauses{} verified checkers), relapse at an 8B operating point climbs from \ScaleDelayedMTwo{} to \ScaleDelayedMEight{} as constraint load grows, while stronger models sit at floor. Under matched checkers, model, and budget, ahead-of-time compilation significantly reduces relapse against a no-ledger verifier-retry baseline (\RestoreDiff{}, 95\% CI \RestoreDiffCI{}, $p$ \RestoreDiffP{}); adaptive ladder interventions stacked on top add no detectable gain (95\% confidence excludes gains $\geq$ \LadderExcludedGain{}). The probe predicts relapse before delivery (AUROC \AurocPrimary{}); a one-sentence tombstone note recovers about a third of the compilation effect and survives a placebo control. At \CostDeliveryFactor{} delivery overhead and \CostTotalHedged{} of API compute for every result, revocation failure becomes a measurable, predictable, and repairable property of dialogue state rather than an invisible one.

</details>


### [204] [@skills: Attention is all you have](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12610)
*Li Yin,Zhi Li,Zhan Shi,Haoran Zhang,Haebin Seong,Zhangyang,Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 @skills 开放协议，将技能（skills）的安装过程拆分为内容、持久化和自动触发三个功能，仅自动触发需要提示词（prompt）驻留。通过路径寻址、Git 跟踪和 .gitignore 风格的单行触发声明，实现了"按需读取、即读即用"，无需安装、清单或注册，从而解决了公共技能长尾无法被使用以及团队自有技能与公共技能争夺稀缺触发槽位的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前公共技能数量已达56,804个且团队还大量私有机会，但主导交付模式（安装）存在根本矛盾：安装后技能描述驻留在系统提示词中，竞争不到100个可靠触发槽位，导致长尾技能无实用路径、团队自有剧本也需争夺同一稀缺空间。作者观察到安装实际上捆绑了内容、持久化和自动触发三个可分离的功能，仅最后一个需要提示词驻留。

Method: 提出 @skills 协议，核心设计包括：(1) 路径寻址——路径可指向任意技能、子树或集合；(2) 分离安装的三个功能——读取技能即视为使用，无需安装或驻留；(3) 写入（vendoring）操作将指定路径的副本拷入项目的 Git 跟踪树中以便适配备份；(4) 仅需添加一行 .gitignore 风格的触发声明，这是唯一消耗提示词驻留的元素；(5) 目录即菜单，使技能包变为普通目录而非全有全无的单元；(6) 协议无需清单、锁文件或注册，SKILL.md 保持不变。实现为可安装包，通过单个指令文件即可使任何能读文件和执行命令的智能体成为客户端。另配有可选的免费中心站提供语料库级搜索、排名与托管。

Result: 该协议以加法方式设计、兼容现有技能格式，通过单个指令文件即可部署到任意能读文件、执行命令的智能体上。@skills 配有开放规范仓库（GitHub）和 AdaL CLI 实现，以及可选的免费 hub（atskills.one）提供全库搜索、排名、私有/团队集合和可视化创作。gh: 和本地路径无需 hub 即可解析，已被索引的 GitHub 技能保持原有 gh: 身份。

Conclusion: 通过将提示词驻留成本压缩为单个触发声明行，@skills 实现了"少安装、多使用"的理念，为技能长尾提供了实用路径，同时以增量、无需迁移的方式兼容现有生态，是解决智能体技能可扩展性问题的有效方案。

Abstract: There are 56,804 public agent skills today, and teams write many more privately.
